(cek kuṭiyaracu)

					



iyal – 3

cemmoḻit tamiḻilakkiyac
(caṅka ilakkiyac) ciṟappu

tolkāppiyam

tolkāppiyam “māntaṉ aṟivāṟṟal evvaḷavu viyattaku ucca nilai eytakkūṭum eṉpataik kāṭṭum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu” One of the finest monuments of human intelligence  . ilakkiyak koḷkaiyil vēṟu entap paṇṭaiya moḻikaḷilum illāta ciṟanta karuttukkaḷait tol poruḷ. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟukiṟatu.

kē.vi. cuvelepil

tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyal kōṭpāṭṭuc ciṟappu :

tolkāppiyam aṭippaṭai nūlāka iruppatu ilakkaṇa ilakkiya āyvukaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟu; mānta iṉap paraval cārnta puviyiyal, kumukāya māntaviyal, paṇpāṭu, cūḻnilaiyiyal, uḷaviyal ākiya pulaṅkaḷ cārnta ariya ceytikaḷ annūlil uḷḷaṉa; eṉavē tamiḻar nākarikam- paṇpāṭu cārnta (ēṉ potuvāṉa mānta iṉa nākarikam- paṇpāṭu cārnta) āyvukaḷukkut tolkāppiyam mika iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. eṉiṉum ilakkiyam kūṟuvatellām (akkālattil nilaviya) uṇmaik kumukāya nilaiyai nēraṭiyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal tavaṟu; uṇmai nilaiyai viruppuveṟuppiṉṟi aṟiviyal pūrvamākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal mēlum tavaṟākum. akkāla āṇ peṇmakkaḷiṉ akam, puṟa vāḻkkaiyai uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi tatrupamākac caṅkap pulavarkaḷuṭaiya pāṭalkaḷ kāṭṭuvatākak kumuka varalāṟṟāciriyar cilar muṉṉar koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl uṇmai eṉṉa? ceyyuḷ ‘uṇmai vāḻkkai” nikaḻccikaḷai antanta nikaḻcci eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa ilakkiyamarapu uḷḷatō anta marapukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē cittirikkiṟatu. atāvatu marapukkēṟṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ cittirikkiṟatu. “uṇmai vāḻkkai” vēṟu; atai ilakkiyattil kāṭṭum marapu vēṟu; eṉpataip pulavarkaḷum poruḷilakkaṇavāciriyarum uṇarntiruntaṉar. uṇarntiruntataṉāltāṉ uṇmai vāḻkkaiyiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ ceykaikaḷai ‘ulakiyal vaḻakku” eṉṟum ilakkiyattil (ceyyuḷil) vāḻkkaiyaic cittirikkum marapaic ceyyuḷ vaḻakku “pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku” eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar. cila nikaḻccikaḷ, ceytikaḷai ippaṭi ippaṭittāṉ kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ilakkiyappaṭaip pāḷarkaḷum vācakarkaḷum tamakkuḷ icaintu uruvākkum mēlvaric caṭṭamākiya marapē ilakkiya marapākum. (e.kā. ovvoru tiṇaikkum uriya mutal, karu, uripporuḷkaḷ). nikaḻcci/ ceyti vācakarukkut terintatu tāṉ; ataṉai oru putiya pārvaiyil, kōṇattil (marapai aṭippaṭaiyil mīṟāmal) kāṭṭum poḻutu vācakar anta nayattai naṉku uṇarntu pārāṭṭa iyalkiṟatu. akappāṭal etuvum orutaṭavaikūṭat talaivaṉ, talaivi peyaraic cuṭṭu vatillai. talaivaṉum talaiviyum eppoḻutum iṉṉār eṉṟu kuṟippāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṭṭa muṭiyātavarkaḷē. eṉavē akappāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum uṇarvukaḷ (ṭapiḷpu. ec. haṭcaṉ tamatu “ilakkiyap payiṟcikkāṉa muṉṉurai” 1946 nūlpakkam 97il colvatu pōla) “potuvāka enta āṭavaṉukkum peṇṇukkum poruntuvatāka irukkumēyaṉṟi ivaṉukkut tāṉ/ivaḷukkuttāṉ poruntum eṉṟu irukkātu. (uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷai viḷakkātu) veḷiyulakai viritturaikkum puṟappāṭalkaḷil cila potuvāka aṉaivarum nōkkum pārvaiyil irukkum; cila antantap pulavaruṭaiya taṉi nōkkil amaintatāka irukkum. piṉṉavaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kaiyaṟunilaip pāṭalkaḷ. vaḷḷalkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ iṟantāl pulavarkaḷ tam neñciṉ āḻattiliruntu uṇarvukaḷaik koṭṭip pāṭum tuyarap pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉittaṉmai vāyntavai; potuvāṉa nāṭṭuppuṟa oppārikaḷiṉ vālāyap paṭimaṅkaḷaik koṇṭavaiyalla”.

kē.vi. cuvelepil

2. tirukkuṟaḷ

“intiya makkaḷiṭaiyē paṇṭaik kālattiliruntē piṟa uyirkaḷ aṉaittukkum aṉpukāṭṭi utavum aṟak koḷkaiṉa (the idea of active love) ” iruntu vantatai, intiyap paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum kāṇum kataikaḷil iruntu aṟikiṟōm. attuṭaṉ kuṟippākat tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūlil kāṇum aṟavuraic ceyyuḷkaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm.

“(ulakil piṟa uyirkaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭic ceyalpaṭuvatai (life Affirmation) avvaḷavāka valiyuṟuttāta maṉutarma cāttiram pōṉṟavaṟṟiliruntu māṟupaṭum) kuṟaḷil ulakaiyum vāḻkkaiyaiyum veṟuttu otukkum pōkku (World and life negation) paravalākak kāṇappaṭuvatu alla. eṅkō ōriru iṭattil vēṇṭumāṉāl irukkalām.

“pirāmaṇīyam, puttamatam pakavatkītai pōṉṟavaṟṟaip pōl immai maṟumaip payaṉ karutiyē aṟaneṟiyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vāṇikak koḷkaiyaik kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttuvatu illai. nallatu ceyvatē takkatu eṉṟa uṇarviṉālēyē nallatu (aṟam) ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kuṟaḷ kūṟukiṟatu (kuṟaḷkaḷ 222,211 pakavatkītaiyō karumam ceytu koṇṭiruppatu pirapañcaniyati eṉṟu vaṟaṭṭut taṉamāka valintu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, eṉṉa viyappu, kuṟaḷō maṉitaṉ uḻaippatum īṭṭuvatum piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum avaṉ naṉmai ceyvataṟkākattāṉ eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṟatu. (kuṟaḷkaḷ 81,212) pakavat kītaippaṭi kaṭamai jātikku takkapaṭi vēṟupaṭum. kuṟaḷō makkaḷ aṉaivarum ‘nallavai ceytoḻukak kūṟukiṟatu’.

“ēṉai intiya tarmacāstirakkārar uḻaippatil, muyaṟci ceyvatil makiḻvōṭu īṭupaṭu eṉṟu kūṟavillai. kuṟaḷkaḷ 619,630 pōṉṟavaiyō ulakiyalilum, vāḻkkaiyilum, ārvattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭum paṭiaṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.”

“(pauttamum kītaiyum pōlap paṟṟiṉmai, veṟuppiṉmai, kollāmai, akimcai ākiyavaṟṟaiyum kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttiṉālum kūṭa atē camayattil) kuṟaḷ atikamāka valiyuṟuttuvatu aṉpum aruḷumē yām (the living ethic of love). kāṇka kuṟaḷkaḷ 72,78,79,103226,241.” “aṟaneṟicār ulaka vāḻkkai ilaṭciyattait tiṟampaṭac cuṭṭukiṟatu kuṟaḷ. maṉitaṉiṉ taṉi vāḻkkai. piṟa uyirkaḷōṭum ulakattōṭum uṟaḻum avaṉ vāḻkkai ākiyavaṟṟil kaikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya neṟikaḷaik kuṟaḷ viyattaku paṇpuṭaṉum cālpuṭaṉum naṭaimuṟaikku kanta vakaiyil vakuttuḷḷatu ((characterised by nobility and good sense). ulaka ilakkiyattil vēṟu eṅkum ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oḷimayamāṉa aṟavurai vācakaṅkaḷai vaḻaṅkum nūlaik kāṇa iyalātu; (kuṟaḷkaḷ 92, 105, 108, 121, 159, 162, 216, 298, 319, 578,594, 628, 757, 782, 874, 931, 973, 999, 1007, 1024, 1032”

“āka kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkak kālattilēyē intiya makkaḷiṭaiyē ulakavāḻkkaiyiṭamum uyirkaḷiṭamum paṇpum aṉpum kāṭṭuma oru taṉmai iyalpāka iruntu vantuḷḷataik kuṟaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pirāmaṇiyam, pauttam, pakavatkītai cār intu matam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷil illātatu atu. āṉāl kīḻcātiyiṉariṭamum, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṭamum tōṉṟi makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāka vāḻnta ciṟanta camayap pōtakarkaḷ mūlamāka attaṉmai metumetuvāka nāḷaṭaivil intu matattil iṭampeṟalāyiṟṟu.” 

- ālparṭ cuvaiṭcar: intiyac cintaṉaiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyum Indian Thought and its development; 1936

3. eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukaḷ (vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illātavai) “eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭu nūlkaḷ (mottam 2300 pāṭalkaḷ) acciṭṭu ulakiṉ pārvaikku mutalil vantatu 1880 - 1910 kāla aḷavil ākum. avai veḷivanta akkaṇamē (atuvarai eḷitāṉa piṟkāla intiya moḻikaḷil
oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭa nilai māṟi ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻi kaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻ ākiviṭṭatu. pireñcu āciyaviyalaṟiñar piyar meyl “kirēkka uṇarccip pākkaḷil talai ciṟanta navamaṇikaḷukku iṇaiyāṉavai iccaṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ; intiyāvil, ēṉ ulakil, ilakkiyap paṭaippukaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu caṅka ilakkiyam” eṉṟu ceytuḷḷa matippīṭṭai caṅkailakkiyap pāṭalkaḷai āḻntu payiṉṟuḷḷa evarum ēṟṟuk koḷvar.”

- cuvelapil (1960)

 “ulakiṉ nākarikam - paṇpāṭṭukkut tirāviṭarkaḷ, kuṟippākat tamiḻarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa ciṟanta paṅkaḷippukaḷ pallava, cōḻar kālak kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭakkalai; cōḻar kālac ceppup paṭimaṅkaḷ; parata nāṭṭiyam eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum tamiḻar naṭaṉakkalai; karunāṭaka icai eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum tamiḻ icai mutaliya palavām; avaṟṟuḷḷum talaimai cāṉṟa ciṟappu vāyntatu (caṅka kālac cemmoḻit tamiḻ) ilakkiyam. piṟamoḻi, piṟa nāṭṭu aṟiñar itai uṇarntu ivvilakkiyattaip payiṉṟu ulaka mānta iṉattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta uyariya marapuc celvamāka makiḻntu pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. uccanilaikku muḻut takutiyuṭaiya avvilakkiyattiṟku itu varai annilai tarappaṭavillai.”
- cuvelapil (1973)

“intiyāviṉ piṟa enta moḻi ilakkiyattilum illāta ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷaik koṇṭatu eṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟip paṇṭait tamiḻ (caṅka) ilakkiyam uyariya ilakkiya nayamum vāyntatu. ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷ varicaiyil tamiḻukkuriya iṭattaik kaḻaka ilakkiyattiṉ 26350 varikaḷ koṇṭa pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟut tantuviṭṭaṉa. avvilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappai ippoḻututāṉ ulaku uṇarat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.”

“acai, cīr, taḷai aṭippaṭaiyilāṉa yāppiyal, akam puṟak kōṭpāṭu; uvamaviyal, ākiya aṉaittilum taṉit taṉmai vāynta ciṟanta ilakkiyak koḷkai koṇṭa kaḻaka nūlkaḷai uruvākkiya perumai tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭukku maṭṭumē uriya ciṟappākum.

“intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta cevviyal ilakkiyam - cevviyal moḻip perum paṇpāṭukaḷ iraṇṭu – oṉṟu camaṟkirutam; maṟṟatu tamiḻ; avai oṉṟai oṉṟu cārntavai yalla - taṟcārpāṉavai.

 “intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyē tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē cevvilakkiyamākavum, nikaḻkāla ilakkiyamākavum orē nērattil ilaṅkum ciṟappu vāyntatu; atu camaṟkirutac cevviyal ilakkiyam aḷavu toṉmai vāyntatu; paṇṭai kirēkka moḻic ceyyuḷkaḷai eppaṭiccevvilakkiyam eṉa ulaku karutukiṟatō atē taṉmai vāyntatu tamiḻk kaḻaka ilakkiyam. uyirt tuṭippuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uruvākum moḻiyākavum tamiḻ uḷḷatu.

“tamiḻc cevvilakkiyam (caṅka ilakkiyam) makkaḷaip paṟṟiya ilakkiyam; makkaḷ uruvākkiyatu – āṉāl “nāṭṭuppuṟa” ilakkiyamaṉṟu” 	
- cuvelapil (1973) 

“vāḻkkai iṉiyatu; makiḻcci taruvatu. cāvu el lōrukkum varuvatu. ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu viṭutalai peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyellām illai. vaḷḷaṇmaiyum nayaṉmaiyum iyalpāka amaikiṉṟaṉa - ātaṉiya (āṉmīka) nalaṉukkākavō kaḻuvāyākavō alla. iṟantapiṉṉar vīrarum cāṉṟōrum tuṟakkattil nilaiyāka vāḻvar; tīyōr mīḷānirayam eytuvar eṉak karutiṉar. nila ulakil pukaḻai niṟīic ceṉṟavar tuṟakkattil iṉiyavāḻvu eṉṉum vekumati peṟuvar: kātal, puṇarcci, tirumaṇam, makkaṭpēṟu ivai mānta vāḻvil niṟaivu peṟuvataṟkāṉa vaḻimuṟaikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷḷaṉmai, vīracceyal, piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉāka vāḻtal - ivaṟṟil oruvaṉ celaviṭṭa nāṭkaḷai niṉaittup pulavarum piṟarum kūṟum pukaḻ moḻikaḷ maṭṭumē oruvaṉ cāvukkup piṉṉarnilainiṟpavaiyākum.” 
- cuvelapil (1974)

“kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ekkālattukkum poruntum aḻakiyal kūṟukaḷ, viḻumaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai nirampak koṇṭavai. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar yātta avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil (atāvatu aṇmaikkālat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar) vakuttuḷḷa ilakkiya nayakkōṭpāṭukaḷiṉ paṭiyum uyarnilaiyil ēṟkappaṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.

“kaḻaka ilakkiyattaip payilum oru kumukāyamē (taṉi napar maṭṭumalla) avvilakkiya viḻumaṅkaḷai eyta iyalum vakaiyil, eyta viḻaiyum vakaiyil avvilakkiyam amaintuḷḷatu. eṉavē tāṉ ikkāla ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvāḷarum pōṟṟum vakaiyil ulaka māntar aṉaivarum matikkat takka eṉṟu muḷa (cirañcīvi) ttaṉmai koṇṭuḷḷatu kaḻakailakkiyam”
- cuvelapil (1974)

“paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyamum paṇpāṭum paṭaitta akam - puṟakkōṭpāṭukaḷ ulakilēyē taṉittaṉmaiyum ciṟappum vāyntavai. cila poruṇmaikaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattukkum camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattukkum oppumai kāṇappaṭalām. paṭṭāl eṉṉa? camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiliruntu tāṉ tamiḻ ilakkiyam kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkum eṉa ēṉ karuta vēṇṭum? (nērmāṟākavum nikaḻntirukkalām aṉṟō?)”
- cuvelapil (1975)

“potuvāṉa ceytiyait teḷivāka, muḻumaiyāṉa nuṇṇiya pārvaiyil nōkki, eḷiya āṉāl vayiram pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil, āḻnta poruḷuṭaṉ taruvatē ciṟanta pāṭal eṉa ikkālattil karutappaṭukiṟatu. veḷippaṭaiyāṉa orē ceytiyait taruvataiviṭa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa pala poruḷait taravallatāṉa coṟceṭṭumikka pāṭalē ciṟanta pānalat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tolkāppiyam nōkku, payaṉ, eccam (colleccam,kuṟippeccam) uḷḷuṟai, iṟaicci eṉṟu kūṟuvatellām itu tāṉ;
- pi. marutanāyakam (2007)

“āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyāvai, ēṉ perumaḷavukku āriyarukku muntiya intiyāvaiyēvivarikkum  72  ulakaaṟiñarkaḷ pārvaiyil tamiḻ āvaṇam puṟanāṉūṟu a testament of pre- Aryan South India and, to a significant extent of pre - Aryan India.” 

“mutalāvatāka, tamiḻiṉ toṉmai kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ikkālattiya piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ōrāyiram āṇṭu muṉṉar uruvāṉa ilakkiyattaik koṇṭatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉāti nūl ākiya tolkāppiyattiṉ pakutikaḷ ki.mu. 200ai oṭṭiyavai eṉpatu mikap paḻaiya satamiḻik] kalveṭṭukaḷiliruntu terikiṟatu. paḻantamiḻp perunūl kaḷāṉa eṭṭuttokai pattuppāṭṭu mutaliyavai ki.pi oṉṟu - iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷaic cārntavai. intiyāviṉ miku toṉmai vāynta, camayam cārāta (secular) pāṭalkaḷ avaiyē; kāḷitācaṉ ilacciyaṅkaḷukku irunūṟu āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭavai.” 

“iraṇṭāvatāka, tamiḻ ilakkiyamarapu maṭṭum tāṉ (camaṟkirutattiliruntu peṟappaṭāta) intiya maṇṇukkuriya ilakkiya marapu ākum. teṉṉāṭṭil camaṟkirutat tākkam valuppaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē mukiḻttatu atu. camaṟkiruta/piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉmai yuṭaiyatu atu. tamiḻ taṉakkeṉṟu taṉi ilakkiyak koḷkai, ilakkaṇamarapu, murukiyal (aesthetics) uṭaiyatu; ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil amainta tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam vēṟu emmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta taṉittaṉmai vāyntatu (unique). camaṟkirutattilō vēṟu intiya moḻi kaḷilō uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa uṇarvai (sensibility) - intiya maṇṇukkē uriya uṇarvaip paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil kāṇalām; tamiḻppulavarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅkālam vaḷartteṭutta ceḻumaiyāṉa uṇarvu marapunilai atu.

“mūṉṟāvatāka, camaṟkirutam - ilattīṉ, cīṉam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟukku iṇaiyāṉa taram vāyntatu tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam. avvilakkiyam nuṇmāṇuḻaipulamikkatu; palvēṟu pāṭu poruḷ koṇṭatu (muṟkāla intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil potu makkaḷaip paṟṟiyum niṟaiyap pāṭiyatu tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē); mānta iṉattukkup potumaiyāṉa viḻumiyaṅkaḷaikkūṟiyatu. eṉavē piṟa cevviyal moḻikaḷiṉ marapukaḷuṭaṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷuṭaṉum camamāka niṟkum uraṉ vāyntatu atu. ulakiṉ talaiciṟanta aṟanūlkaḷil oṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ. tamiḻc cevviyal ilakkiyattil atu pōṉṟu pala tuṟaikaḷilum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. avvilakkiyam pāṭit tulakkam tarāta mānta iṉa paṭṭaṟivu oṉṟum illai.

“iṟutiyāka ikkāla intiyap paṇpāṭṭaiyum marapaiyum uṇarvataṟkup payilavēṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻilakkiyam mutaṉmai vāyntatu. camaṟkiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil teṉṉaka maraput tākkam uḷḷatu kuṟittunāṉ virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. kaḻakakkālam toṭaṅkit tamiḻilil pāṭappaṭṭa ciṟanta intu camaya patti ilakkiya nūlkaḷ tām taṟpōtaiya intu camayattukku aṭittaḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ patti ilakkiyak karuttukaḷ pākavarāṇattilum piṟa (teluṅku/kaṉṉaṭa/ camaṟkiruta) patti nūlkaḷilum ēṟṟappaṭṭu intiyā muḻuvatum paraviṉa. vētattukkuc camamākak karutappaṭum tamiḻ tivya pirapanta patti ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil periya tirumāl kōyilkaḷil (tiruppati pōṉṟavai) vētattōṭu cērttuōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāla intō āriyamoḻikaḷukku camaṟkirutamē mūlamoḻi; atupōla ikkālat tamiḻukkum malaiyāḷattukkum cevviyal tamiḻē mūla moḻiyākum.

“ulakattiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil tamiḻum oṉṟu eṉpatu ittuṟaiyil valla aṟiñarukku uḷḷaṅkainellikkaṉi pōlat terinta uṇmaiyākum”

- jārj el.hārṭ 11.4.2000
(i. maṟaimalai vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅka varaintatu)

 “caṅka ilakkiyam mutal kamparāmāyaṇam muṭiyavuḷḷa cevviyal tamiḻ ilakkiyam mānta iṉaccātaṉaikaḷuḷ mikac ciṟantavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. karuttukaḷ, ilakkiya amaippu, coṟkaḷañciyam ākiyavaṟṟiṟku iṉṟaiya tamiḻp paṭaippulakattukku caṅka ilakkiyam vaṟṟāta karuvūlamāka uḷḷatu. teṉ āciyāvil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum vēṟu enta
moḻikkum illāta taṉicciṟappu itu. paḻaiya cevviyal ilakkiyattai aṭittaḷamākak koṇṭē ikkālattamiḻ ilakkiyam uruvākiyuḷḷatu; iṉṟaiya tamiḻppaṭaippāḷikaḷ (vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyam mēlai nāṭṭilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟaiyum uḷaṅ koṇṭirunta pōtilum teḷivākauṇarntu koṇṭō allatu āḻmaṉanilaiyil uḷḷatākkattālō tamiḻc cevvilakkiyattaiyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tāṉ uḷḷaṉar. iṉṟaiya tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ nām kāṇum coṟkaḷañciyam, kumutāya/ meyyiyal/ camaya paṇpāṭṭu nōkku, toṉi (tone) ākiyavaṟṟil perum pakutiyiṉ vērkaḷ ōraḷavukku caṅka ilakkiyam varaic celvataik kāṇalām. taṅkaḷ paṭaippāṟṟalai viyakkattakka aḷavukku mēmpaṭuttakkūṭiya ceḻumaiyāṉa paltuṟaivaḷaṅkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyak karuvūlattil uḷḷaṉa eṉpatai iṉṟaiya tamiḻ eḻuttāḷarkaḷ uṇarntatākat teriyavillai; uṇarntāl atil atika nāṭṭam celuttuvar, eṉpataiyum nāṉ ivvēḷaiyil collāmalirukka muṭiyavillai.”
āṣar (2004)

- “Negotiaions with the past: Classical Tamil in contemporary Tamil; Edrs Kannan M and Carlos Mena Institute Francais de Pondichery”

caṅka kāla akapporuḷ nūlkaḷiṉ taṉmai

“toṉmaiyāṉavaiyē eṉiṉum iṉṟum uyirūṭṭam uḷḷavaiyāy ilaṅkum inta (caṅka ilakkiya) akap pāṭal kaḷaip pōṉṟa nayam vāyntavai intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu evaṟṟilum illai. appāṭalkaḷ kūṟum vāḻkkai neṟiyilum, viḷakkum nikaḻccikaḷilum ciṟanta cemmoḻi ilakkiyattiṉ piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa: kātalōṭu kaṉi vum paṇpāṭum; veḷippaṭaik kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu uḷḷuṟai, iṟaicci, aṅkatam ākiyavaiyum; talaivaṉ, talaivi peyar cuṭṭappaṭāviṭiṉum ōviyam pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya vaṇṇaṉai; aṭikaḷ cila avai cuṭṭum poruḷō peritu. tamiḻar aṟivuttiṟaṉiṉ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu ivvakappāṭalkaḷ; atu maṭṭumaṉṟu kaṭanta īrāyiram āṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyap paṭaippil ivaṟṟai viñcuvaṉa illai.”
- ē.kē.irāmāṉucaṉ

“akappāṭal ovvoṉṟum oru kūṟṟu: talaivaṉ, talaivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliya cilaruḷ oruvar, nāṭaka māntarpōl, kūṟuvatāka atu amaiyum. avarkaḷ oruvaruk koruvarō, taṉakkut tāṉākavō, nilavai nōkkiyō kūṟuvatai vācakarkaḷākiya nām kēṭkiṟōm; avvaḷavē. nāṭakak kāṭci oṉṟai muḻumaiyāka akappāṭal oṉṟil uḷḷa kūṟṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭiviṭum.ivai nāṭṭuppāṭalkaḷ pōl viraintu pāṭiyavai alla; oru nikaḻcciyaik kaṇṭa allatu eṇṇippārtta oru pāvalar atu paṟṟiya tam eṇṇaṅkaḷai nērākavō urumāṟṟiyō veḷippaṭuttiya pāṭalkaḷum alla. ivai aracavaippāṭalkaḷiṉ naḷiṉam uṭaiyavai; “vaikalum eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ tiṅkaḷ valitta kāl pōl” nuṇṇiya pulamaittiṟaṉuṭaṉyākkappaṭṭavai.
- cuvelapil

4. tamiḻar, paṇpāṭu, kalai,
camayam, meyyiyal

iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum nākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavai uṇmaiyil tamiḻar, tamiḻiya nākarikak kūṟukaḷē “intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi - paṇpāṭu ākiyavaiyē” eṉpar ṭailar.

All of Indian civilisation is built on an underlying base of Dravidian language and culture” - S.A. Tyler (1973) India an Anthropological perspective.

1. cintuveḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam

tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Ascent) kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyil uruvākkiyatu cintuveḷi nākarikam, annākarikam 1947 kku muntiya intiyā, pākistāṉ, vaṅkāḷa tēcam cērnta intiyāviṉ parappaḷavil kāl pakutikku mēl parappaḷavu koṇṭa perunilappakutiyil vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil paravi iruntatu. atāvatu cumār 5 laṭcam caturamail parappaḷavil vaḻaṅkiyatu. paṇṭaiya mecapotāmiya, ekiptiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappaiviṭa atikamāṉa parappil cintu veḷi nākarikam vaḻaṅkiyatu. annākarikam vaḻaṅkiya pakutiyil ki.mu.2000 vākkil mottam aintiliruntu pattu laṭcam makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉpatu posel, parpōlā pōṉṟavarkaḷ karuttu ākum (appoḻutu ulakil irunta motta makkaḷ tokaiyē 9 kōṭikku kuṟaivu). cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 1500 kkum mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 20 iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē mokeñcotarō, harappā uṭpaṭa –akaḻvāyvu niṟuviyuḷḷatu. annākarikattiṉ ciṟappuṟṟa nilai ki.mu. 3200 - 1800 kāla aḷavaic cārntatu. mokañcacōtarō nakara makkaḷ tokai 40,000 eṉṟum harappā nakara makkaḷ
tokai 25000 eṉṟum matippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. māntar iṉa (AMH) paraval, moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyē cintu veḷi nākarikap pakuti eṉṟu Dravidian Ascent mēlē kaṇṭōm.

annākarikam tirāviṭac cārpuṭaiyatu eṉpataṟku uḷḷa mēlum pala valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷil cila varumāṟu :-

i. mokañcotarō - harappā iṭipāṭukaḷil tirāviṭak kaṭṭumāṉak kalaiyaik kāṇalām. vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nakaikaḷ, parutti āṭaikaḷ aṉaittum tirāviṭac cārpuṭaiyavai.

caṅka nūlkaḷil kāṇum kaṭṭaṭakkalai cintuveḷi nākarikak kaṭṭaṭakkalaiyiṉ toṭarcciyē. eṭai aḷavukaḷ; ciṟṟilakka muṟai; 4.ce.mī (= aṅkulam) kōl; 2 3/4 X  4 = 11 ākum 11,22,33, eṉṟavāṟu 11ṉ maṭaṅkāka varum aḷavukaḷ, pōṉṟavai yellām cintuveḷik kālattiliruntu toṭarntu varupavaiyē (es. caṇmukam 1980; ku. vēṅkaṭācalam 1983, teyva nāyakam 2002)

ii. tamiḻariṉ ciṟṟilakka muṟaiyaiyē cintuveḷi nākarika eṭai aḷavukaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉpataic ceṅkam ku. vēṅkaṭācalam niṟuviyuḷḷār.

iii. iṉṟaiya vētamatak karuttukkaḷ – avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa, puttam mutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭaiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇaippu eṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉa talla. cintuveḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭaiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē, maṟumalarcciyē iṉṟaiya intu matam ākum.

(The Hindu synthesis was less the dialectical reductionof orthodozy and heterodoxy than the resurgence of the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)

maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1903, 1923,1930) pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār 1929 ākiyōr ikkaruttiṉarē, 

iv. iṉṟaiya icaikkalai āriyar tamiḻariṭamiruntu kaṟṟatē (ō.kōsvāmi “intiya icai varalāṟu”, 1957)

v. cintuveḷi muttiraikaḷ cumār mūvāyirattil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ paḻantamiḻē (paḻantirāviṭamē) eṉpatai ittuṟai vallunar aṉaivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. (avarkaḷuḷ iṉṟuḷḷa varkaḷ askō parpōlā, ai.makātēvaṉ, kamil cuvelapil, posel) cintuveḷi muttirai eḻuttukkaḷait tol tamiḻ ākappaṭittuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ vācittuḷḷa mātirikaḷ iṟutiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaṟiñarkaḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoru vakaiyāka vācittuḷḷaṉar. muttiraiyaik kuttiyapiṉ kiṭaikkum muttiraippativil uḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷai (impression) valamirunatu iṭamākavē perumpālōr paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. (aṟiñar mativāṇaṉ iṭamiruntu valamākap paṭittuḷḷār) eṉiṉum im muttirai eḻuttukkaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻiyaic cārntatākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉtu paṟṟi maṭṭum aṟiñarkaḷiṭam oru mitta karuttu uḷḷatu kāṇka :-

sṭāṉlivālparṭ : intiyāvukku ōr aṟimukam “pala vakaiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷiliruntum nām karutuvatu avarkaḷ tol tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻukkup pāṭṭaṉ muṟaiyākak kūṭiya oru moḻi avarkaḷ moḻiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉpatu ākum.”

“We assume from various shreds of evidence that they were Proto - Dravidian possibly using a language that was a grandfather of modern Tamil” (Stanley Wolpert : An Introduction to India, University of California press 1991)

jē.em. rāparṭs : peruṅkuyiṉ ulaka varalāṟu “teṉṉintiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷōṭu iyaipuṭaiya oru moḻiyaic cārntavaiyāka (cintuveḷi muttiraic coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used in Southern India (J.M. RobertsHistory of the world, pelican 1992)

kamil cuvelapil: tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal – ōr aṟimukam “ cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvap paṭumpoḻutu atu tirāviṭamoḻi cārntatāka amaivataṟkē vāyppu mika atikam”

“The most probable candidate is and remains some form of Dravidian” (Dravidian Linguistics - An introduction, Pondicherry 1990;) Chap VI : Dravidian and Harappan)

cintu veḷi muttirai eḻuttukkaḷiliruntē piṟkālat tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ uruvāyiṉ eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ karuttu, ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.700 ai oṭṭiyē tamiḻukku oru lipi iruntatu; ataippiṉ paṟṟiyē acōkapirami eḻuttu uruvākkap paṭṭatu; eṉak koḷvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷai rājaṉ (2004) naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr tantuḷḷaṉar.  aṇmaiyil kaṇṭupiṭitta āticca nallūr akaḻvāyvup pāṉai oṭṭiṉ kālamum ki.mu. 700kku muntiyatu.

2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟai – cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putuk kaṟkālak karuvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷ nāṉku uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai ai. makātēvaṉ “murukuaṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār. akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000 - 1500 āka irukkalām eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. “putuk kaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orē moḻiyaiyē pēciṉar eṉṟum ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē ik kaṇṭupiṭippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpar makātēvaṉ.”

(“The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu and the Indus vallyey people shared the same language which can only be Dravidian and not Indo- Aryan”) : 1.5.2006 intu nāḷitaḻ.

2. camayam, meyyiyal

(iṉṟaiya “intu” camayakkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ, meyyiyal ākiyavai muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭac) cārpuṭaiyavaiyē āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavai alla eṉpatai 1900kku muṉṉar iruntē nallaṟiñar kūṟuvarukiṉṟaṉar. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1903 : mullaippāṭṭu ārāycciyurai, 1923 tamiḻar nākarikam, 1941 tamiḻar matam); pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār (1929: History of the Tamils kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai (1940 tamiḻar camayam) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (1972 : tamiḻar matam) ākiyōr uṭpaṭa. āriya vētaṅkaḷi liruntu māṟupaṭṭa, avaṟṟukku muntiya ‘tamiḻ nāṉmaṟaikaḷ’ iruntaṉa veṉṉum kōṭpāṭṭaiyum kā. cuppiramaṇiyar (1927 : The Madras Christian College Magazine, piṉṉar 1929 (centamiḻccelvi) mutaliyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar.

 	“aṟiyāmai kāraṇamāka ‘ āriya’ meyyil, nākarikam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭuvatu, aṭippaṭaiyil tirāviṭar allatu tamiḻaruṭaiyatu eṉpatē uṇmai. Most of what is ignorantly called Aryan philosophy and civilisation is literallyDravidian or Tamilian at bottom..” maṉōṉmaṇiyam pe.cuntarampiḷḷai. 19.12.1896 kaṭitam) : Siddhantha Deepika , 11- 5; Oct 1898 P .113).

rikvēta kālattiliruntē (ki.mu. 1000) “kuṭi kolai malinta āriya vēḷviyiṉ tīviṉaip peṟṟiyuñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkac ciṟumaiyun tēṟṟi aṉpum aruḷum perukac
ceyta tamiḻc cāṉṟōrum uḷar. avar aruḷic ceytaṉavē; muttī vēḷviyum uruttira vaḻipāṭuñ ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟum irukku, ēkar, cāma atarvaṇa vētappatikaṅkaḷ cila palavum; uṇmai viḻā upaniṭataṅkaḷum, cāṅkiya, yōka, vaicēṭika, naiyāyika, vētānta cūttiraṅkaḷum; maṟcam, vāyu mutalāṉa cila purāṇaṅkaḷum pauṭkaram, mirukēntiram mutalāṉacila civākamaṅkaḷum piṟavumām.”

-maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ;
māṇikkavācakar varalāṟum kālamum (1930)

“irukku vētattiṟ “paratar” mutaliya peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paṇṭait tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ , tī eṉṉum muttī vaṭivil vaittu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civa pirāṉai vaḻipaṭum nuṭpa muṟaiyaik kaṇṭōrāvar.(aṭikaḷ : 1930)”

“vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaracu muṉivarāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta mūṉṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantiramāṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷaittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya civapirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu” (aṭikaḷ : 1923)

“vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaraca muṉivarāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta mūṉṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantiramāṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷaittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya civapirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu” (aṭikaḷ : 1923)

“kollā aṟattai mutaṉ mutaṟkaṇṭaṟintu ataṟkēṟpat tamatu immai vāḻkkaiyai naṭattiṉavarkaḷ tamiḻaril mēṉ makkaḷāy irunta oru perumpakutiyārē... maṟṟai makkaṭ piriviṉariluḷḷaenta mēlōrum ivvarumaruntaṉṉa uṇmaiyiṉaik kaṇṭaṟintilar - vaṭakkirunta camaṇar, cākkiyar, cāṅkiyar, yōkar mutalāyiṉāruṅ kollā vaṟattiṇai viṭāppiṭiyāyk koṇṭa oḻukiṉarallarō veṉil; camaṇa cākkiyar mutalāṉa avarellām āriyar varutaṟku muṉṉamē imayamalai varaiyiṟ paravi nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu uyir vāḻnta vaṭanāṭṭut tamiḻmēṉ makkaḷiṉ marapil vantavarē” aṭikaḷ (1940) 

[camaṇa, puttakkaruttukkaḷ āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭanāṭṭuttirāviṭa meyyiyaliṉ piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷē eṉpar car jāṉ mārṣalum (1934).]

“tamiḻar paṇṭaic camaya vāḻvu iṉṟaiya pala camayaṅkaḷiṉ uruvākā mūlak karu mutalait taṉṉiṭamākak koṇṭa vāḻvēyākum. atil ellāc camaya mūlamum kāṇalām. putta camaṇa, caiva, vaiṇavak karuttukkaḷ uruvākumuṉ, accamayap peyarkaḷ vēṟuvēṟākat tamiḻar neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piriyumuṉ, āriyam iṭainiṉṟu tamiḻariṉ oru neṟiyaip palavāṟu pirikkumuṉ, avvellā neṟikaḷukkum vaḻikāṭṭiya mika ēṟpaṭṭa kālat tamiḻ aṟivar marapu cārnta aṟiñaruḷ oruvarē tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉpatu uyttuṇarat takka uṇmaiyākum. “kā. appātturaiyār; 1956 (“ 1800 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakam”) tamiḻākkattiṉ aṭikkuṟippu.

pirākkiṅṭaṉ (1981) : intiyāvil camaya vaḷarcciyil kuṟippiṭattakkatu oṉṟu uṇṭu uyirkaḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṟaviyeṭukkum eṉpatu putta matattiṉ mukkiya koḷkai. puttamatam tōṉṟiya kālattil tōṉṟiya camaṇamum ācīkavamum akkoḷkaiyuṭaiyavaiyē. piṟavittaḷaiyiliruntu viṭupaṭuvatu camayattiṉ kuṟikkōḷ eṉpatu ammataṅkaḷiṉ koḷkai.”

“paḻaiya upaniṭataṅkaḷil maṟupiṟavik koḷkai putumaiyāṉatākavum cilarukku maṭṭum terinta maṟai poruḷākavum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu iruntatu. iruntum piṉṉar akkoḷkai putta camaya cayamaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka viraintu aṉaivarālum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu evvāṟu? enta makkaḷ camūkattiṉariṭam iruntu puttar tōṉṟiṉārō, avarkaḷiṭaiyē akkoḷkaiyāka iruntu irukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāṉ atu viraivil aṉaittu makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.”
J.L. Brockington 1981 : The Sacred thread

heṉric cimmar (1951) : “vaṭamēṟkiliruntu nuḻainta āciriyarkaḷāl makatam mutaliya kīḻtticai nāṭukaḷilirunta āriyarallāta mēṉmakkaḷ muḻumaiyāka iṭam peyarntilar. attakaiya mēṉ makkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ valuviḻantu vīḻat toṭaṅkiya piṉ muntaiya uḷḷūr āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ mīṇṭum valuppeṟṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka cantirakup mauriyaṉ ittakaiya kuṭumpattaic cārntavaṉ. puttarum avvāṟē.

- Heinricimmer 1951: Philosophies of India

leviṉ (1986) : “intu camayattil yāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum uruva vaḻipāṭu intiyāvil irunta āriyar allātār vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷil iruntē pirāmaṇīyattukku vantatu”

G.M. Bongard Levin (1986) A complex study of Ancient India

vālparṭ (1982) : mokañcōtarō muttiraiyil kāṇappaṭupavarpōṉṟa (āriyarkaḷukku muntaiya intiya) yōkikaḷ, vantēṟikaḷ moḻiyaic cila kālattukkuḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭu tamatu camaya maṟai poruṭkaḷai (vētap pāṭalkaḷai iyaṟṟiya) pirāmaṇarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉarō)

- Stanley Wolpert 1982: A new history of India.

vālparṭ (1991) : āriya nāṭōṭikaḷiṉ muraṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭaiya kaṭumaiyāṉa perum pōrkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkum ahimcaikkum (uyirkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyāmai) neṭuntolaivu ākaiyāl, yōkam, tāytteyva vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟaippōla ahimcaiyum āriyarkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa (cintu veḷittamiḻ) makkaḷiṭa miruntē avarkaḷ peṟṟatāka nām karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.

“civaṉ vaḻipāṭuvaṭa intiyāvai viṭa teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ makkaḷiṭam mutaṉmai peṟṟuḷḷatu. teṉṉāṭṭut tirāviṭa makkaḷ āriyarkaḷukku neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉarē aṅku nilaipeṟṟu iruntamaiyai itu kāṭṭuvatākalām. tāytteyva vaḻipāṭum teṉṉāṭṭiltāṉ mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu”

- Stanley Wolpert (1991) An introduction to India.

3. naṭaṉamum icaiyum

(tamiḻar naṭaṉamum tamiḻicaiyum iṉṟu karunāṭaka icai, paratanāṭṭiyam eṉṟa peyaril vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.) 

“pāṇar tamiḻariṭaiyē toṉṟutoṭṭu irunta varupavar; camutāyattil tāḻnta nailayil iruntavar; eṉavē paṇ aṭippaṭaiyil amainta teṉṉintiyat toṉmai icai vaṭavariṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟatāka irukka iyalavē iyalātu. “paṇ eṉṉum colliliruntu “pāṇaṉ” eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyatu. māṟākap piṟkāla intiya icaiyiṉ rākaṅkaḷ (avai paṇkaḷiṉ urumāṟṟaṅkaḷō) teṉṉintiyat tol icaiyiliruntu uruvāyiṉa eṉpatē cari. takkāṇap peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikam cārnta vāymoḻi ilakkiyamāṉatu pāṇarkaḷ maṟṟum eḻuttaṟivu peṟāta nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭakarkaḷiṭaiyē uruvākik ki.pi.1 mutal nūṟṟāṇṭai oṭṭi makāraṣṭirap pirākirutattil paravi, aṅkiruntu ivvilakkiyattiṉ tākkam camasrutak kaviñarkaḷiṭam ēṟpaṭṭataṉ kāraṇattāl tāṉ camaskirutattil taramāṉa kāviyac ceyyuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatai ipputtakattiṉ iraṇṭām pakutiyil kāṇalām. ikkāla kaṭṭattil allatu itaṟkuc caṟṟu muṉṉart teṉ intiya icaikkalaiyum vaṭa intiyāvil paraviyirukka vēṇṭum eṉa eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu itu paṟṟi virivāṉa, āḻnta āyvukaḷ ituvarai ceyyap paṭavillaiyākaiyāl itaittiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa muṭivu eṉṟu iṉṟaiya nilaiyil kūṟa iyalātu. ikkaruttiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil viyakkattakkatum maṉa niṟaivu taruvatumāṉa oru ceytiveḷivarukiṟatu. atāvatu teṉ intiyac cātiyiṉaril tāḻnilaiyil irunta pāṇariṭaiyē iruntu tāṉ intiyāviṉicaiyum pāṭalkaḷum perumaḷavu uruvāyiṉa eṉṉum ceytiyē atu ” 

- ji.el. hārṭ “paḻantamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷ:
avai tōṉṟiya cūḻalum, avaṟṟai
aṭiyoṟṟiya camaṟkirutap paṭaippukaḷum” 1975)

“icaiyaṟivai āriyarallātavarkaḷiṭamiruntu āriyar kaṭaṉ peṟṟatai maṟaippataṟkākavē, cāmavētattiliruntu intiya icai piṟantatu eṉa avarkaḷ katai puṉaintaṉar.”

“By this fiction that Saman is the source of all Indian music alone it was possible to forget conveniently the debt the Aryans owed to the non- Aryans for their musical knowledge and practice.”

- ō. kōsvāmi (1957) History of Indian music

iṇaippu I

ē.ṭi. kāmpel 1816il veḷiyiṭṭa “teluṅku moḻi ilakkaṇam” eṉṉum nūlukkut tāṉē oru aṟimukavurai Introduction  varaintatuṭaṉ tamatu naṇpar paṉmoḻi aṟiñar pirāṉcis vaiṭ ellis avarkaḷiṭam oru kuṟippaiyum Note to the introduction eḻuti vāṅki acciṭṭār. akkuṟippaip pārāṭṭi avar eḻutiyatu: “teluṅku moḻiyiṉ amaippu, ammoḻiyiṉ pūrvikam paṟṟiya eṉatu muṉṉuraik karuttukaḷukku valuvūṭṭu vataṟkāka eṉ naṇpar pirāṉcis vaiṭ ellis (puṉitar jārj kōṭṭaik kallūri mēlāṇmaik kuḻuttalaivar) aṉpuṭaṉ eḻutiyutaviya piṉvarum āyvuraiyai makiḻvuṭaṉ acciṭukiṟēṉ. teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷil mukkiyamāṉavai aṉaittilum āḻnta ñāṉam uṭaiya avar camskirutattilum pulamai peṟṟavar ākaiyāl ivviṣayattil tīrnta muṭivu ceppiṭa avar muḻuttakuti vāyntavar āvār.”

ellis tanta kuṟippu
Note to the Introduction - by Ellis

teluṅku moḻi enta moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārntatu eṉpatu paṟṟi ituvarai āyntōr evarum uṇmai nilaiyaik kaṇṭaṟintilar. ṭākṭar kāri Carey camskiruta ilakkaṇa muṉṉuraiyil kūṟuvatu:

“hintustāṉiyum, tamiḻum, kujarāt nāṭṭu moḻiyum, malaiyāḷa nāṭṭu moḻiyum, camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉavai eṉpatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu; āyiṉum mutaliraṇṭu moḻikaḷil camskirutam allāta piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ perum eṇṇikkaiyil kalantuḷḷaṉa. peṅkāli, oricā,marāttā, karnāṭaka, teliṅka moḻikaḷaip poṟuttavaraiyil avaiyaṉaittumē ēṟattāḻa muṟṟilum camskirutac coṟkaḷaik koṇṭavaiyē”

aṇmaiyil kāri veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa teluṅku ilakkaṇattiṉ muṉṉuraiyil avar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:

“intiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittumē perumaḷavukku camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉavaiyē eṉalām. mattiya intiyāvilum vaṭa intiyāvilum vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṉ amaippum structure potuvāka orē mātiri uḷḷatu; avaṟṟiṭaiyē kāṇum māṟupāṭukaḷ perumpālum (i) peyarccol / viṉaiccol. vikuti māṟṟaṅkaḷum, (ii) camskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ eḻuttamaippil antantap piratēca makkaḷiṭaiyē kālappōkkil uruvāṉa vēṟupāṭu kaḷumēyām. teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ ākiya teliṅka, karnāṭik, tamiḻ, malaiyāḷa, ciṅkaḷa moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷaip pōṉṟatēyeṉiṉum avaṟṟiliruntu cila viṣayaṅkaḷil māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa - kuṟippāka enta moḻi vērkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu aṟiya iyalāta ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷai avai koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.”

ṭākṭar vilkiṉs-um ikkaruttai vaḻimoḻintu.

“tamiḻ, teluṅku, karnāṭik, malapār moḻi kaḷilum marāttā, kujarāt pakuti moḻikaḷilum camskirutac coṟkaḷ perumaḷavukku uḷḷaṉaca mskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟi immoḻikaḷil oru vākkiyam kūṭa amaikka muṭiyātu eṉṉum aḷavukku” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. Asiatick Researches āyvitaḻ 7ām maṭalattil kōlpurūk eḻutiya “camskiruta – pirākiruta moḻikaḷ paṟṟiya āyvurai” yil kāri, vilkiṉsa pōṉṟavar kaḷaip pōla tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka muṭivu kūṟavillai eṉiṉum avarkaḷ muṭivai ātarippatu pōlavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. mēlum camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉa / amaikkappaṭṭa moḻikaḷai maṭṭumē cērkka vēṇṭiya “pirākiruta moḻi kaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalil” avar teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷaiyum cērttuḷḷār. taṟpōtaiya tēvanākari lipiyiliruntu uruvāki tiripaṭaintatu tāṉ tamiḻ lipi eṉpatum, “karṉāṭa”,
“teliṅkāṉa” moḻi lipikaḷum tēvanākariyiliruntu piṟantavai eṉpatum kōlpurūk karuttukaḷ. (tirunelvēliyil ōṭum āṟṟiṉ camskirutap peyar āṉa)tāmraparṇi eṉṉum peyariliruntutāṉ tamlā Tamla eṉṉum col uruvāṉatāka ūkittu accollāl tamiḻ moḻiyaic cuṭṭukiṟār. uṇmaiyil kaṉṉaṭa, teluṅku lipikaḷ (iraṇṭum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟutāṉ) nākari oḻuṅku muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉum eḻuttuvaṭivam, kūṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivam mutaliyaṉavaṟṟil avai nākari lipiyaip pōṉṟavai alla; tamiḻō nākari lipiyiliruntu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭatu -- piṟaṅkeḻuttukaḷ aṟavē illai; mēlum camskirutam eḻutappaṭum enta lipiyilum eḻutaviyalāta ciṟappu olikaḷaik koṇṭatu tamiḻ.

2. inta āyvuk kuṟippiṉ nōkkam muṉpattiyiṟ cuṭṭiya karuttukaḷ piḻaiyāṉavai eṉpataik kāṭṭuvatutāṉ. attuṭaṉ piṉvaruvaṉavum niṟuvappaṭum - atāvatu, tamiḻō, teluṅkō avaṟṟiṉ iṉamoḻikaḷō camskirutattiliruntu kiḷaittu uruvāṉavai alla. camskirutam immoḻikaḷai merukūṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭirunta pōtilum avaṟṟiṉ iruppukkut (nilaippukkut) tēvaiyaṟṟatu; avai oru taṉiyāṉa moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārntavai. ammoḻikaḷil camskirutac coṟkaḷ (kuṟippākap piṟkālaṅkaḷil) kalantuḷḷa pōtilum vērccoṟkaḷ nilaiyil avaṟṟōṭu camaskirutattukkut toṭarpillai.

3. “teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpam”eṉṟu poruttamāṉa peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkattakkavaiyāṉa ikkuṭumpattil aṭaṅkiya moḻikaḷ: centamiḻum vaḻakkut tamiḻum High and low Tamil; teluṅku (paṇṭita naṭaiyum pāmarar naṭaiyum Grammatical and vulgar); karṉāṭakā allatu kaṉṉaṭi(paḻaiya moḻiyum iṉṟaiya moḻiyum) malaiyāḷmā allatu malaiyāḷam; (ceṉṉai rājatāṉippaṭattil kaṉṉaṭa Canara māvaṭṭam eṉa vaḻaṅkum pakutiyil uḷḷa tuḷuvam ākiyavaiyām. (malaiyāḷattai pāliṉas, pari partolomiyō ākiyavarkaḷ iraṇṭākap pirippar: camskiruta (āṭampara) malaiyāḷam, grandonico Malabarica, cātāraṇa malaiyāḷam eṉṟu. eṉiṉum piṉṉatiliruntu muṉṉatu vēṟupaṭuvatu cakaṭṭu mēṉikku camskirutac coṟkaḷaip pukuttuvatil maṭṭumtāṉ.

4. mēṟcoṉṉavaṟṟōṭu ikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷākac cērkkattakkavai cila uṇṭu: kuṭaku (Coorg) māvaṭṭattil vaḻaṅkuvatum tuḷu moḻiyiṉ kiḷaimoḻiyum āṉa “kuṭaku” moḻi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. ciṅkaḷam, makārāṣṭiram, oṭiya moḻi ākiyavai im moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārntaṉavalla; eṉiṉum teṉṉintiya moḻic coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷuḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṇṭai nāṭukaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṭaiyē (avai vevvēṟu kuṭumpa moḻikaḷāka iruppiṉum,) ōraḷavu moḻik kalappu ēṟpaṭṭuttāṉ tīrum. āṉāl vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa nākarikamaṭaiyāta malaippakuti vāḻ iṉakkuḻuviṉariṉ moḻikaḷilum teṉṉintiya intukkaḷiṉ moḻik kuṭumpac cāyal iruppatu viyappukkuriyatu. rājmakāl pakuti malaivāḻ paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ moḻiyāṉatu teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārntatāka irukka vēṇṭum. oruvēḷai atai niṟuva iyalāviṭṭālum tamiḻ/teluṅkuc coṟkaḷaiyotta coṟkaḷaip perum eṇṇikkaiyil ammoḻi koṇṭuḷḷatu eṉpatu uṇmai.

5. ivvāyvurai kuṟippākat teluṅku paṟṟiyatu. ammoḻic coṟkaḷiṉ vērkaḷ ataṉ iṉa moḻikaḷāṉa tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ vērkaḷaip pōṉṟē uḷḷaṉa; (ciṟcila cillarai oli māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ) camskirutattuṭaṉō vēṟu enta moḻiyuṭaṉumō toṭarpaṟṟavai. teluṅku, tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭam immūṉṟu moḻic coṟkaḷiṭaiyē nām kāṇum vēṟupāṭāṉatu vērkaḷiliruntu uruvākkappaṭum col vaṭivaṅkaḷil uḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ tām; mūṉṟilum vērkaḷ ottuḷḷaṉa. ēṉ vērkaḷ oṉṟē eṉalām. the roots themselves are not similar merely, but the same.

6. teluṅku vērkaḷ (camskiruta vērkaḷaip pōla) perumpālum viṉaikaḷē; avviṉaikaḷai appaṭiyē payaṉpaṭuttalām; allatu ciṟu vikuti oṉṟaic cērttu peyarc col allatu peyaraṭaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttalām. perum pālāṉavai appaṭi peyarccol/peyaraṭaiyākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa:-

kuttaṭamu (viṉaiccol) to strike with the fist
kuttu guddu (peyarccol) a blow with the fist
naṭavaṭamu(vi) to walk
naṭaka (pe) nadaka a step, progress, conduct, manner vērkaḷaip palavakaiyilum payaṉpaṭuttum vitaṅkaḷil immoḻikaḷ māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa; tamiḻilum teluṅkilum peyarccol ākap payaṉpaṭum vēr oṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattil viṉaiyākap payaṉpaṭum (allatu itaṟku nērmāṟākavum amaiyum);

tamiḻ akkaṟai

eṉakku akkaṟaiyillai (It is not a want to me / I do not require it) 
kaṉṉaṭam akkaṟīyi  (to be desired / to be endeared to)
pala nērvukaḷil oru moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷai viḷakkum vēr ammoḻiyil illāviṭiṉum cakōtara moḻiyil irukkum:-

teluṅku akappaṭaṭamu (to take)

ati tūmamukā nāk akappaṭinti (I take it to be smoke)
tāṉikinantamū nā ka(g)ppaṭalētu (I do not take or comprehend the sense of it)
tamiḻ: akappaṭu = to take in general, seize, obtain
kuruvi eṉakkakappaṭṭatu (I have caught the bird) 
(teluṅkil aga, agu  ākiyavaṟṟukku taṉinilaiyil poruḷ illai. āyiṉum tamiḻil akam = interior; mēlum tamiḻ, teluṅku iraṇṭilum paṭu = to suffer)

7. camskirutattukkum teluṅkukkum vērccol nilaiyil toṭarpu illai eṉpatai kāṭṭu mukattāṉ piṉ varum paṭṭiyalilē-. a, ka, pa,va ivaṟṟil toṭaṅkum camskiruta tātu pāṭa mālai yiliruntu eṭutta nāṟpatu ( 4 X 10 ) vērkaḷuṭaṉ paṭṭāpirāma cāstiri (puṉita jārj kallūri talaimai camskiruta - teluṅku paṇṭitar)
tokutta teluṅku tātu tātumālaiyiliruntu eṭutta nāṟpatu vērkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. appaṭṭiyalaip pārtta aḷavilēyē teluṅku vērc coṟkaḷ 40-il oṉṟiṉ poruḷ oṉṟukūṭa camskaruta vērc collukku porunti varavillai eṉpatu teḷivākat teriyum. kuṟippu: aṭ+ eṉa immūṉṟu moḻikaḷilum toṭaṅkum vērkaḷaṉaittukkum mūlam inta aṭu eṉpatē; avvērkaḷaṉaittilum nerukkam, iṇaippu, ivai cārnta poruḷkaḷē cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūla vēril piṉvarum ciṟumāṟṟaṅkaḷ poruḷ māṟṟattukku vakai ceykiṉṟaṉa:-

iṟuti acaiyiṉ muṉ mūkkoli cērttal aṇṭu (antu,andu)

palvēṟu oṭṭukaḷaic (ai,i, ar, al, ku, aṅku pōṉṟavai) cērttal –
mēlē kaṇṭatu pōla.

ippaṭi (akaram, kakaram mutaliya enta eḻuttil toṭaṅkum) enta aṭippaṭai vērilum ciṟu ciṟu māṟutalkaḷaic ceytu ērāḷamāṉa iraṇṭām nilai vērc coṟkaḷai uruvākkum muṟaiyaip paravalākak kāṇalām. ātivēr cila nēram tamiḻ/kaṉṉaṭam/teluṅku immūṉṟil oṉṟilum, cila nēram im mūṉṟu moḻikaḷilum
kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila vēḷaikaḷil ātivēr iṉṟu immūṉṟu moḻikaḷil etilum kāṇappaṭumāṟillai.

ippaṭi am moḻikaḷiṭaiyē vērccol nilaiyil neruṅkiya uṟavu uḷḷatai niṟuva muṭiyum eṉṟālum ammoḻikaḷil palvēṟu poruṇmaikaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷiṭaiyē neruṅkiya oppumai iruppatāka eṭutta eṭuppil tōṉṟātu. eṉavē itai (vērccol oṟṟumai) tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuva vēṇṭumāyiṉ immūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷaiyum vērkaḷaiyum oppiṭṭup pārppatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. māmiṭi veṅkayyā āntira tīpikā eṉṟa peyaril arumaiyāṉa teluṅku akarāti oṉṟai eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avvakarātiyiṉ muṉṉuraiyil teluṅku moḻiyiṉ amaippaic curukkamāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār. mēṟcoṉṉa oppīṭṭaic ceytiṭa utavum vakaiyil veṅkayyāviṉ anta viḷakkam āṅkilattil tarappaṭukiṟatu;

“āntira (teluṅku) moḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ mūlaṅkaḷ nāṉku vakaiyiṉa. avaiyāvaṉa: tatcaman, tatpavan, tēsyam, kirāmyam.

teluṅkil vaḻaṅkum tūya camskirutac coṟkaḷ.

tatpavac coṟkaḷ

camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyākavō allatu piṉvarum āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷ vaḻiyākavō kaṭaṉ peṟṟu cila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ payaṉpaṭuttupavai “tatpavam” ākum. ammāṟṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa: cila acaikaḷai nuḻaittal, eḻuttukkaḷai māṟṟutal, tēyttal allatu nīṭṭutal pōṉṟavaiyām. (vivaraṅkaḷai vaikrūta cantirikā vil kāṇka. eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ piṉvarum paṭṭiyalkaḷil tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.)

apirā nāṭṭilum mēlaik kaṭaṟkaraiyilum vaḻaṅkum āpapramsa pirākirutattiṉ mūlamākak kaṭaṉpeṟṟa tatpavaṅkaḷ :

heard 

kuṟippu: āpapramsam eṉṟa collukku vērp poruḷ ‘citainta moḻi’ corrupted language eṉpatākum. āyiṉum intap poruḷil accollai eṭuttuk koḷḷak kūṭātu eṉṟu māmiṭi veṅkayyā kūṟukiṟār. oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭāra vaḻakkiṉ iyaṟpeyarāka ataṉaik koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉkiṟār. naṉṉayapaṭṭiya nūlukku uraiyeḻutiya āppakaviyiṉ pāṭal oṉṟil ikkaruttu uḷḷataiyum ‘carasvati tēvi ciṟumiyāka irunta pōtu pēciya moḻiyē atu’ eṉavē ammoḻiyil uḷḷa aṉaittuc coṟkaḷum cuttamāṉavaiyē eṉṟu appāṭal kūṟuvataiyum cuṭṭukiṟār. eṉiṉum āpapramsam mūlamākat teluṅkil nuḻainta coṟkaḷaip pirāmaṇarkaḷai viṭa cūtramakkaḷē atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu teluṅkil nuḻainta tatcamac coṟkaḷiṉ vakaippāṭu varumāṟu. avaṟṟaic citaikkap paṭṭa coṟkaḷ eṉpataiviṭa urumāṟiya coṟkaḷ eṉpatē poruttam. camaskiruta tatpavam 1/2, pirākirutam 1/4, cauracēṉi 1/10, mākati 1/20; paicāci, cūlika āpapramsam 1/10, “camaskirutam - pirākirutam paṟṟiya tamatu āyvurai”yil kōlpurūk camaskirutam, pirākirutam, mākati (=āpapramsam) ākiya mūṉṟu vakaippāṭukaḷaiyēēṟkiṟār. mēlē (māmiṭi veṅkaiyā coṉṉa) āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷum taṉit taṉimoḻikaḷ. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟumē tattamakku ukanta ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, 

camaskirutac coṟ kaḷañciyam
camaskirutat toṭaramaippu
camaskirutailakkaṇam - cila vitivilakkukaḷukkuṭpaṭṭu.

ākiyavaṟṟaip piṉpaṟṟi uruvāṉavaiyēyām. laṭcumitaraṉ āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷukkum potuvāka eḻutiya satpāṣā cantirikā (Shadbasha - Chandrica) vāṉatu mutalil āṟu
pirākirutaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷait tarukiṟatu; piṉṉar “camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyāka uruvākiyatākiya” pirākirutattiṉ (prākrutam mahārāṣṭira utpavam) vitikaḷait tarukiṟatu; piṉṉar “camskirutam = pirākirutam” ivaṟṟiliruntu uruvākiya cauracēṉi paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē aṭuttaṭuttu oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka uruvāṉavaiyāka mākati, paicāci, cūlikapaicāci, maṟṟum āpapramsa ākiyavai paṟṟi kūṟukiṟatu. oṉṟiliruntu aṭuttaṭuttu paṭippaṭiyāka cuttam kuṟaintatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu; ellāvaṟṟaiyumviṭa āpapramsamtāṉ camskirutattiliruntu mikutiyum vēṟupaṭṭatu. kōlpurūk āpapramsattai muṟaiyāṉa ilakkaṇamaṟṟa koccai moḻi a jargon destitute of regular grammar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭiruntār; māṟāka laṭcumītaraṉ piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭu ataṟkum ilakkaṇa vitikaḷai vakukkiṟār:

āpapramsamoḻi apira nāṭṭilum piṟanāṭukaḷilum pēcappaṭum moḻiyākum; atu kavikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum moḻiyākaliṉ atu evvakaiyilum citaivuṟṟatākak karutattakkataṉṟu

apapramcastu pāṣā syāt apirā
kirañcayakavi prayōkaner hetvān
na apacaptas sa tu kvacit

mēṟcoṉṉasatpāṣā cantirikā iṉṟuḷḷa (camskiruta) nāṭakanūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭum mēṟkaṇṭa vakaip pirākirutaṅ kaḷai maṭṭumē viḷakkuvatāl, atu viḷakkuvatellām
camskirutattiliruntu peṟṟa tatcamaṅkaḷaiyum, taṟpavaṅkaḷaiyum maṭṭumē. eṉiṉum pirākirutaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟumē camskirutattiliruntu peṟṟa tatcama, tatpavacoṟkaḷōṭukūṭa antanta pirākirutam vaḻaṅkiya nāṭṭukkēyuriya tēciya Desyam coṟkaḷaiyum koṇṭiruntaṉaveṉpataiyum teḷivākak kūṟukiṟatu. oṉṟukkoṉṟu tolaivilirunta “pirākiruta” nāṭukaḷiṉ “tēciya” moḻikaḷ oṉṟuk koṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭamaiyāl tatcama, tatpavac coṟkaḷōṭu tattam tēciyac coṟkaḷaiyum kalantu antanta tēcaṅkaḷil uruvākiya pēccumoḻikaḷ. oṉṟukkoṉṟu perumaḷavukku vēṟupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṟatu. paicāciyaip poṟutta varai inta nilaimai piṉvarumāṟu mikat teḷivāka viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu.

“intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyiluḷḷa pāṇṭiya, kēkaya, cūlika, tēcaṅkaḷ maṟṟum cahya (cayām) nēpāḷa, kuntaḷa, cutēca, pōta, kāntāra (vaṭakkil) kaṉōja (kaṉōjaṉa)
ākiya nāṭukaḷilum iruvakaip paicāci moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa; ivaiyellām paicāca tēcaṅkaḷ;  antanta tēcattiṉ paicāca moḻiyil uḷḷa tēciyac coṟkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa tēca moḻiyiṉ taṉmaikkēṟpa amaintuḷḷaṉa.

“āntira (allatu teluṅku) moḻiyiṉ tēciyac coṟkaḷ iruvakaippaṭum: mutal vakai teliṅkāṉa tēcattilēyē uruvāṉavai; iraṇṭāvatu anniya tēciyam atāvatu piṟanāṭukaḷil uruvāki (antanta pirākirutattiṉ oru kūṟāka) teluṅkil pukuntavai.

tiriliṅka tēcattil uruvāṉa tēciyac coṟkaḷ

“teluṅku moḻiyil evvevai tēciyac coṟkaḷ eṉṟu collumuṉṉar tiriliṅka tēcattiṉ ellaikaḷaik kūṟum ataravaṉa viyākaraṇattiṉ piṉvarum pāṭalkaḷaik kāṇka. [ellis antap pāṭalkaḷait tām acciṭavillai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuviṭṭut toṭarkiṟār]tiriliṅka tēcattiṉ ellaikaḷaip piṉ varumāṟu avviyākaraṇa āciriyar varaiyaṟukkiṟār:

‘cirīcailam, tirāṭcārāmattil pīmēsvara nkṣatram, mahā kāḷēsvaram, nālāvatāka mahēntiramalai ākiyavaṟṟukkiṭaiyē mūṉṟu liṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. tiriliṅka tēcam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa inta tēcattil uruvāṉa moḻiyaiyē iṅku kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. atuvē acca teluku. appakāviyap pāṭal itaip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟatu.

“āntira piratēca pūrva kuṭikaḷāṉa cakala jātiyiṉariṭaiyēyum vaḻaṅki varum teḷivāṉavaiyum (tōṟṟam kuṟitta) aiyattiṟkiṭamillātavaiyumāṉa coṟkaḷ ellām cutta āntira tēciyac coṟkaḷ ākum.

avaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:-

piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu teluṅkil nuḻainta coṟkaḷaip paṟṟi

appā kāvīyap pāṭal kūṟuvatu:

“ō kēcavā! āntira tēcattavar piṟa tēcaṅkaḷil vacittu anta tēcac coṟkaḷaiyum teluṅkuṭaṉ kalantu puḻaṅkiyatāl ivai teluṅkil ēṟiya ‘anniya tēcac coṟkaḷ’ āyiṉa.

“āntiram, atāvatu tiriliṅkattaic cārnta makkaḷ, āppakavi colvatu pōla, piṟa tēcaṅkaḷil vacittu antanta tēcac coṟkaḷaiyum teluṅkil cērttuk koṇṭaṉar. attakaiya anniya tēsyac coṟkaḷil cilavaṟṟaik kāṇpōm.

(i) cutta teluṅkiṟku uriya 30 eḻuttukkaḷil aṭaṅkāta ha (aspirate) meyyoliyaik koṇṭa coṟkaḷ: bhale palē an eulogistic exclamation; avatāru /( avadharu) an exclamation of entreaty; tāvu (thavu) a place - station,  tāka (dhaca) a haughty, high spirited man;; [ ivaṟṟiṉ vērkaḷ teḷivākat teriyavillai.]

(ii) col iṟutiyil neṭil uyir uḷḷa coṟkaḷ: aṇā the sixteenth of a rupee, navalā an excellent woman,, koṭī a flag; jirā armour[ivaṟṟil cila intustāṉic coṟkaḷ; ēṉaiyavaṟṟil kaṭaici uyir taṟceyalāka neṭilākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu ] kōṭiyum koṭiyum oṉṟutāṉ. navalā eṉpatu camskiruta nava (=putiya) allatu tamiḻ navam (= affection) liruntu uruvākiyirukkalām.

(iii) kaṭaiciyāka kalanu (= battle) toyyali (= a woman) mēnu (= the body) uḷḷamu ( = athe mind) ākiya kaṭiṉamāṉa coṟkaḷ anniya tēcac coṟkaḷākat tavaṟākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ivaipōṉṟa coṟkaḷ teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉavai:

tamiḻ : kala - to join - kal - kalanu ( kala tamiḻ, teluṅku,
kaṉṉaṭam mūṉṟukkum potuvāṉa vēr.)
tamiḻ : tai - to beautify - taiyal - toyyali
tamiḻ: mēl-  upward / outward - mēṉi - mēnu
tamiḻ: uḷ inward / mind [uḷḷam ] - uḷḷamu
kirāmiya makkaḷ, pāmarar vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ

ivai, potuvāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku aṭaṅkātavai; aṟiyāmai kāraṇamākap putiya eḻuttuk kaḷaip pukuttiya, allatu paḻaiya eḻuttukkaḷai nīkkiya kirāmiyac coṟkaḷē. avai citainta coṟkaḷ.appakāviyap pāṭal avaṟṟaip piṉvarumāṟuviḷakkukiṟatu:

“kirāmattu makkaḷ potuvāka vaḻaṅkum teṉukuc coṟkaḷ kirāmyac coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil cila eḻuttukkaḷ nīkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccoṟkaḷai ceyyuḷil potuvākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatillai – vacai kūṟum poḻutu nīkka muṭivatillaiyākaiyāl vacaic coṟkaḷil maṭṭum avai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

“vastāṭu 		hari 			cōmulu

(vatsūnnāṇṭā - vastāṭā;
telcuccunāṇṭā - testāṭā
cūtcutcunnāṇṭā - cūstāṭā

ceppumu - ceppamu)

mēlē kaṇṭavaṟṟil māmiṭi veṅkaiyā (tamakku muntaiya ilakkaṇakkārar ātāraṅkaḷaiyum cuṭṭi) colvatu eṉṉa? camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyākavum maṟaimukamākavum teluṅku peṟṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷaiyum, ēṉaiya ayalmoḻikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷaiyum nīkki viṭṭāl mīti irukkum coṟkaḷ tēcattiṉ cuttamāṉa tēciyac coṟkaḷām eṉpatē atu. teluṅkuc
coṟkaḷil perumpālāṉavai ittakaiya cutta teluṅkuc coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟaik koṇṭē entak karuttaiyum viḷakkalām; uṭaliṉ ceyal etaṉaiyum vivarikkalām; enta uṟavaiyum, poruḷaiyum kuṟippiṭalām -- matam cārnta cila coṟkaḷaiyum, cila toḻil nuṭpac coṟkaḷaiyum tavira, camskirutattiliruntu piṟanta vēṟu entac coṟkaḷum teluṅku moḻikaku iṉṟiyamaiyātavai alla.

ippaṭippaṭṭa “tūya teṉṉāṭṭu moḻi” teluṅku , tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭam pōṉṟa teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉatākum - mēmpōkkāṉa cila vēṟupāṭukaḷukkum col īṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷukkum uṭpaṭṭu āppakāviya ttiliruntu eṭuttu māmiṭi veṅkayyā cērttuḷḷa teluṅku tēsyac coṟkaḷai atē poruḷ kaḷukkuriya tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭac coṟkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu itaṉai nilaināṭṭalām. immūṉṟu moḻikaḷumē potuvāṉa vērccoṟkaḷaik (cilavaṟṟil maṭṭum ciṟu ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷ) koṇṭavai eṉpatu mēlē niṟuvappaṭṭatu; im mummoḻikaḷiṉ potuvaḻakku tēciyac coṟkaḷum oṉṟu pōlavē iruppataik kāṇpōm.

piṉvaruvaṉa maṭṭumaṉṟi immūṉṟu moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa pōluḷḷa ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷākat tantirukkalām; eṉiṉum teluṅkil potuvāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭuvatum camskirutattiṉpāl allatu tēciya moḻikaḷiṉpāl pārapaṭcam kāṭṭuvatākakkuṟai kūṟavoṇṇātatum āṉa veṅkayyā nūlil uḷḷa piṉvaruvaṉa maṭṭumē tarappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avar camskiruta vallunar eṉiṉum teluṅkait tavirat teṉṉintiya moḻi vēṟu etaṉaiyum aṟintavar alla.

alukkā vexation 							alappu (īṟṟil

teluṅku moḻiyil aṭaṅkiya coṟkaḷiṉ taṉmaikaḷai mēlē pārttōm. accoṟkaḷaip avaṟṟiṉ piṟappuaṭippaṭaiyil piṉvarumāṟu nāṉku pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām:

1) tēsyam / acca teluku: ivai tūya teluṅkuc coṟkaḷ teluṅkukku aṭippaṭai ivaiyē. ivaiyum ivaiyotta coṟkaḷum teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa piṟa
moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa.

2) anya tēsyam (piṟa tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟavai): ivaiyum perumaḷavukku, mutal vakaiyaip pōṉṟavaiyēyām.

3) tatcamam ( tūya camaskirutam) : ivaṟṟiṉ col īṟukaḷil maṭṭum camskirutattai nīkkit teluṅku īṟukaḷaic cērttuḷḷaṉar.

4) tatpavam (camskirutattiliruntu kaṭaṉ): ivai camskirutattiliruntu nērākavō, pirākirutaṅkaḷ āṟil oṉṟu mūlamākavō kaṭaṉ peṟṟavai, ivaiyaṉaittum atikamākavō kuṟaivākavō citaikkappaṭṭavai.

[ “kirāmya”c coṟkaḷai(kirāmam (camsa) - ūr; atāvatu pāmarar coṟkaḷ) taṉippirivākak kūṟa iyalātu; acca teluṅkuc coṟkaḷ curukkiyō allatu (ilakkaṇa muṟaikku māṟāṉa) olimāṟṟam ceytō vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirāmiyac coṟkaḷil 1/2 paṅku acca teluṅku; 1/10 paṅku anya tēsyam; 3/20 paṅku tatcamam; 1/4 paṅku tatpavam]

tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ amaippum teluṅku pōltāṉ uḷḷaṉa; eṉavē ammoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇavāciriyarkaḷum teluṅkaip pōlavē coṟkaḷai nāṉkākap pakukkiṉṟaṉar.

tamiḻai viṭa tārāḷamāka teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum tatcamac coṟkaḷai ēṟkiṉṟaṉa; avviru moḻikaḷilum eḻutupavar tamviruppattiṟkēṟpa ettaṉai viḻukkāṭu vēṇṭumāṉālum camskirutac coṟkaḷait tatcamamākap payaṉpaṭuttalām. āṉāl tamiḻil pulamai naṭaiyaip poṟuttavarai nikaṇṭukaḷil kāṇpaṉavum tamiḻp paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyavaiyum āṉa tatcamac coṟkaḷai maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttalām eṉṟa varaṉmuṟai uṇṭu. tamiḻ cātāraṇa vaḻakkil ittakaiya varampētumiṉṟi camskirutac tatcamac coṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar - pirāmaṇar perum aḷavilum, cūttirar caṟṟu kuṟaivākavum.

tatpavac coṟkaḷaip poṟuttavarai ēṉaiya moḻikaḷai viṭa kaṉṉaṭam caṟṟu atikamākavum, tamiḻ caṟṟu kuṟaivākavum iccoṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻaip poṟuttavaraiyil camskirutac coṟkaḷ īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷil māṟṟamaṭaivatōṭu niṉṟuviṭāmal vēṟupala māṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyil kaṉaippoliha ēṟiya mey illai; camskiruta varkka eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutal maṟṟum mūṉṟāvatu meykaḷaik kuṟikka orē eḻuttē tamiḻil payaṉpaṭukiṟatu; meykaḷ tāmē iraṭṭum poḻutum ṅ ka eṉpatu pōla valliṉattiṉ iṉamāṉa melliṉam maṭṭum cērntu varum poḻutum tavira (kta; ṭca;pōla) piṟa meykaḷ cērntu vara iyalātu. ākavētāṉ mikac cila camskirutac coṟkaḷ maṭṭumē māṟṟamiṉṟit tatcamam ākat tamiḻil nuḻaiya muṭiyum; ēṉaiyavai tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷai maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭutti uriya oli māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ tamiḻil nuḻaiya iyalum; eṉṟālum avvāṟu māṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum tatcamam eṉṟē cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.

perumpālum teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷait tavira vēṟu iṉamoḻikaḷaip poṟutta varaiyil vērkaḷum coṟ kaḷum oṉṟu pōlavē iruppiṉum marapu vaḻakkup poruḷ ‘pala nērvukaḷil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭu irukkum. enta aḷavukku eṉṟāl - iṉa moḻikaḷ iraṇṭil orē mātiri uḷḷa coṟkaḷukku oru moḻiyil uḷḷa arttamtāṉ maṟṟatilum irukkum eṉak karutiṭa iyalātu. āṉāl, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṭaiyē nilaimai appaṭi illai; col aṭukkumuṟai, ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷ, toṭaramaippu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil marapuvaḻakku idiom eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭuvatu avaṟṟiṭaiyē oppumaiyuṭaiyatāka maṭṭumaṉṟi oṉṟupōlavē uḷḷatu. itai niṟuviṭa (atāvatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ tamakkuḷ oṟṟumai yuṭaiyavai, camskirutattiliruntu māṟupaṭṭavai eṉpataik kāṭṭa ṭākṭar vilkiṉs-iṉ camskiruta ilakkaṇattil toṭaramaippu paṟṟiya utāraṇa vākkiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟum ataṉ tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷum kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu nuṇmaiyāṉa ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷilum, ceyappāṭṭu viṉai īṟukaḷ, kālaṅkāṭṭum īṟukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟilum immūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṭaiyēyuḷḷa iyaipu viḷaṅkum.

camskiruta vākkiya amaippu varumāṟu (eṇkaḷ ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum camskiruta vācaka eṇkaḷaic cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa) 

1. peyarccol: eḻuvāy vēṟṟumai, paṉmai [ataṉ viṉai =2]
2. sēratē = he sleeps; paṭarkkaip paṉmai nikaḻkāla viṉaiccol

2. srutam; 2vatu (ceyappaṭuporuḷ) vēṟṟumaiyil peyarccol (eṉṉa ceyyappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatai aṭuttu varum viṉai kāṭṭum)

3. ivvākkiyattil uḷḷa aṉaittuc ceyalpaṭu poruḷkaḷum eṉṉa viṉaikku āḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉpataic cuṭṭum viṉai.(paṭarkkai orumai, nikaḻkālam, Medial voice)

teluṅku vākkiya amaippu

9. (kāvaṭamu = to become) aina (Past participle) camskiruta mētāviyiṉ iṟutiyil cērttu peyaraṭai pōl payaṉpaṭum kūṭṭuccol oṉṟu uruvākkappaṭṭatu.

4. (cams) narō + (te) uṭu = naraṭu: camskirutam 4 pōṉṟatē.

1. camyamāmukoraku: camskirutam 1 pōṉṟatē. camskirutattil vēṟṟumai, eṇ, pāl, ivaṟṟukkāka peyarcol urumāṟṟam declension aṭaiyum. teluṅkil immuṟai illai. āṟām vēṟṟumai urupu ku, -uṭaṉ otta oruku (=for the sake of) cērttu tāṉamu koruku (=tāṉamukōcaram) eṉṟu ākiṟatu.

2. camskirutattil pōl

5,6,8,7,11,12 cams pōltāṉ - āṉāl teluṅku īṟukaḷuṭaṉum oṭṭukaḷuṭaṉum . 11,12 iraṇṭaiyum iṇaikka ovvoṉṟiṉ iṟutiyilum nu yaṉē eṉṉum iṇaippuc col varukiṟatu.

10. cams, pōltāṉ - aṭuttuvarum col ṉāya vil toṭaṅkuvatāl druttam( nunnation)peṟṟuḷḷatu.

3. tariñciccunnāṭu= (camsdharintsadamu to dress - assume eṉṉum colliṉ teluṅku vaṭivam; paṭarkkai orumai nikaḻkālam.

kuṟippu: nāṉkām vēṟṟumaiyāṉa ‘ku’ piṟaṉukkāka’ eṉṟa poruḷil varumpoḻutu compound dative eṉappaṭum. itu āṅkilattil for  eṉṟa muṉṉoṭṭu peṟṟu for x (peyar) eṉṟavāṟu varum. camskirutattil itai tātartya caturtti eṉpar. ta, te, ka moḻikaḷil ku vuṭaṉ, ipporuḷ tarum cila piṉoṭṭukaḷ cērkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa:- ā = be, become āy, āki (viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷ); āka ‘cey’ eṉṉum ecca vaṭivam.

teluṅku: āy, ( to join, obtain oruku, ōcaram )= a side, inclination, bias). orai ( = to join obtain) - oruku) piṉ iraṇṭum āṅkila sake -ṉ poruḷiltāṉ varukiṉṟaṉa. atuvum inta vēṟṟumaikku maṭṭumē. iṉṉārukkāka (for the sake of) eṉṟa coṟṟoṭaraiyum ikkāriyattukku naṉku payaṉpaṭuttalām.

kaṉṉaṭam: āki, ōskaram
tamiḻ: āki, ōcaram

itaṟku muntaiya pāṭṭiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil camskiruta iṇaippiṭap peyar relative clause amaippu, antecident  amaippu ākiyavaṟṟait teṉmoḻikaḷil eppaṭik koṇṭu varuvatu eṉpatu paṟṟip pārttōm. mēṟkaṇṭa camskiruta vācakam varum nūlil atu nāṉkāvatu koṭai vēṟṟumaikkēṟpa palvēṟu viṉaiccoṟkaḷai evvāṟu eḻutuvatu eṉpatai viḷakka vantatu. teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷil varum viṉaikaḷ avvēṟṟumaiyiṉavalla; (cams) ‘upapata tvtya,’ ‘upasarka prati’ ākiyavaiyē varukiṉṟaṉa. ammoḻikaḷil ceyappaṭuporuḷai ‘kuriñci’ allatu kuritu mark, determine eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu nirṇayikkiṟatu. tamiḻilum appaṭiyē amaikkalām. allatu mēluḷḷa tamiḻp peyarppil kaṇṭavāṟu ‘āka’ (evaṉukkāka) eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuc collaip payaṉpaṭuttiyum amaikkalām. [muntaiya utāraṇa viḷakkattil kaṇṭatu pōla]

camskiruta mūlattil kaṇṭa ērāḷamāṉa viṉaiccoṟkaḷukku (ovvoṉṟum nuṇukkamāṉa arttam koṇṭatu) cariyāṉa teṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭippatutāṉ kaṭiṉamāka iruntatu. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭamoḻikaḷil pala karuttukaḷai perumaḷavukkō, kuṟaivākavō camskirutac coṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉālaṉṟi teḷivāka, tiṭṭavaṭṭa mākak kūṟuvatu potuvāka kaṭiṉamākum. tamiḻ appaṭiyalla; atuvum (pulamai vaḻakkāṉa) centamiḻil entak karuttaiyum takavuṟa eḷimaiyākak kūṟalām.ivvutāraṇattil smarah (=maṉmataṉ) - ākupeyarāki kātalaik kuṟittatu; eṉavē sexual love eṉat tamiḻākkiṉēṉ. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭattil attakaiya col illai; “tamakāmu” eṉat teluṅkil varuvatu. kāmam lust -aiyē kuṟikkum; kaṉṉaṭa cokku ācai desire aiyē kuṟikkum.

atupōla, capati he curses eṉpatiliruntu paṭarkkai iṟanta kālam kāṭṭum acapta camskirutattil vantatu. teṉmoḻikaḷ mūṉṟil etilum camskiruta viṉai vēraip payaṉpaṭuttāmal anta arttattai appaṭiyē koṇara muṭiyātu. teluṅku tiṭṭatamu, kaṉṉaṭa pai (b) vatu iraṇṭum vilify, abuse eṉṉum poruḷai maṭṭumē koṇṭavai yeṉiṉum payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. tamiḻilum tiṭṭutal, vaital eṉṟu colliyirukkalām. eṉiṉum avaṟṟaiviṭa poruttamāṉa tūṟṟutal (potuvākap peṇkaḷ vacavukaḷ) eṉṉum collaip peytēṉ. cams hnuti = to dissemble eṉpataṟku teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum camamāṉa col poṅkaṭamu eṉpatākum. tamiḻil (atē vēriliruntu vanta) ‘pokkam’ hnutiyiṉ poruḷai muḻumaiyākat taravillai; atu potuvāṉa vaḻakkil uḷḷa collum alla.

teṉmoḻikaḷil ciṟantavaiyāṉa immūṉṟaiyum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu ottup pārkkavum. avaṟṟai camskirutattōṭu nōkkavum ēntāka āṅkila vākkiyam oṉṟai
immoḻikaḷil tantuḷḷēṉ. (ammoḻikaḷiṉ iyalpai naṉku kāṭṭum vaṭivam ceyyuḷēyākaiyāl avai ceyyuḷil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa) mēlē vivarittavai aṉaittuṭaṉ cērttu avaṟṟaiyum nōkkiṉāl ikkuṟippiṉ toṭakkattil teluṅku emmoḻik kuṭumpattaic cērntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatō atu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.

camskirutam (anuṣṭup vruttam)

kutō pūtvā kuṭa iva vinamya tvam ayō ka(gh)nah pūtvāyōkānavat kataṉām tairyavān prahara tviṣā.

iṇaippu II

tamiḻmoḻipaṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ

heṉṟi ār. hāyciṅṭaṉ (1853)

(hāyciṅṭaṉ yāḻppāṇattil amerikka kiṟittava viṭaiyūḻiyarākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavar. tamiḻaik kaṟṟu am moḻiyilum ilakkiyattilum pulamai peṟṟavar. amerikka kīḻaināṭṭiyal āyvuccaṅkakkūṭṭattil 19.5.1852 aṉṟu amerikkāvil āṟṟiya āṅkila uraiyiṉ tamiḻākkam itu. Journal of the American Oriental Society  itaḻiṉ 3m maṭalattil 1853 m āṇṭil pak 389 - 97 l ikkaṭṭurai veḷivantuḷḷatu. 1856 kālṭuvel oppilakkaṇam veḷivaruvataṟku muṉṉarē nikaḻntatu itu) 

tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ taṉmai

1. tamiḻil iraṇṭu vaḻakkukaḷ allatu kiḷaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻar avaṟṟaic centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉpar; avaṟṟai nām uyarnilaittamiḻ (pulamait tamiḻ) cātāraṇat tamiḻ (High Tamil / Low Tamil) eṉalām.

2. centamiḻ (nayamāṉa, ciṟanta tamiḻ) mūṉṟu pirivu kaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷatu. 1. iyal tamiḻ; iyaṟkaiyāṉa, cariyāṉa tamiḻ. urainaṭaiyil tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷukku
urai eḻutuvataṟku maṭṭumē itu perumaḷavukkup payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. ittakaiya naṭai potuvāṉa pēccu vaḻakkiliruntu māṟupaṭṭu ceyyuḷ naṭaiyai oṭṭiyatāka uḷḷatu. 2. icait tamiḻ; ceyyuḷ naṭaiyil amainta iyal tamiḻē itu. 3. nāṭakattamiḻ; potumakkaḷ muṉ naṭattum nāṭakaṅkaḷil varum eḷiya naṭaippāṭalkaḷai eḻutap payaṉpaṭum iyal tamiḻai itu kuṟikkiṟatu.

3.centamiḻai viṭac ceṟivu. colvaḷam, ekkaruttaiyum veḷiyiṭum āṟṟal, iṉimai ākiyataṉmaikaḷaik koṇṭa moḻi vēṟu etuvum irukka iyalātu. tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tam nūlkaḷai immoḻiyilēyē eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.

4. koṭuntamiḻ eṉpatu potumakkaḷ aṉṟāṭa vaḻakkil payaṉpaṭuttum merukūṭṭappaṭāta tamiḻ ‘koṭuntamiḻ (koṭiya tamiḻ) eṉṟu antavaḻakkaic cuṭṭuvatu poruttamalla. nalla muṟaiyil pēcum poḻutum eḻutum poḻutum atu iṉimaiyum nayamum vāynta tākavē uḷḷatu - tamiḻp pulavarkaḷuṭaiya “aṟappaṭitta” cevikaḷukkut tavira. ekkaruttaiyum collakkūṭiya tiṭpa nuṭpattaip poṟuttavaraiyil centamiḻ aḷavukku atu vaḷam peṟṟatu alla; eṉiṉum ittamiḻilum nuṭpam, āṟṟal, aḻaku ākiyavai miḷirac colla iyalāta entak karuttum illai. entak kuṟaiyumiṉṟi āṟṟoḻukkāka nayamākappēca, eḻutak kūṭiyatākavē atu uḷḷatu.

5. centamiḻ vaḻakkum koṭuntamiḻ vaḻakkum mika vēṟupaṭṭavai. cātāraṇap pēccu/ eḻuttu vaḻakkut tamiḻil muṟṟilum vallavaṉ (kaṭumaiyāṉa pulamai naṭaic) centamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷai muṟaiyē kaṟṟāl oḻiya, avaṟṟait tāṉē vācitta aḷavil oru pakkam – ēṉ (cilanērvukaḷil) oruvarikūṭap purintu koḷḷa muṭiyātu.

tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷ

6. (intiyāvil) tamiḻ nāṭṭilum, ilaṅkaiyilum cērttu mottam tamiḻ pēcuvōr eṇṇikkai (1852 l) 80 laṭcamākum.teṉṉintiyāvil mottam ēṟattāḻa
iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kōṭi pēr pēcum teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam, tuḷuvam maṟṟum cila moḻikaḷait tōṟṟuvittatu tamiḻē eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu; ākavē tamiḻait teṉṉintiyat (toṉ) moḻiyākavē karutalām. tamiḻar itaṉait “teṉmoḻi” (Southern Speech) eṉpar.  vaṭamoḻi eṉa avarkaḷ cuṭṭum camaskirutattiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttuvataṟkāka.

ṅa. tamiḻiṉ varalāṟum piṟa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ataṉ toṭarpum

[viviliya paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil varum nōvāviṉ mūṉṟu makaṉkaḷākiya cēm, hām, yāpēt, Shem, Ham, Japhet ākiyōruḷ oruvaṉākiya] cēmiṉ kuṭi vaḻiyiṉaril
irukiḷaiyiṉar intiyāviṟku vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar – oru pirivu cintuveḷip pakutiyākiya vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟiyatu; maṟṟappirivu teṉmēṟku intiyappakutikkuk kaṭalvaḻiyāka vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatu.

8. iraṇṭāvatu pirivāṉa intō – cemittiyap piriviṉmoḻi tamiḻ ākum. itaṟkup pala ātāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

centamiḻai uruvākkiyavar akattiya muṉi eṉkiṉṟaṉar; avarē ammoḻikku ilakkaṇam eḻuti nayamākkiṉār; pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil aṭaṅkiya potiyamalaiyil avar vacittu vantār. ivvāṟu tamiḻar karutukiṉṟaṉar. (vaṭakkil tipēttum potiyam eṉac cuṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. atu vēṟu, itu vēṟu). intukkaḷiṉ ātikāviyamākiya irāmāyaṇattil, kāviyattalaivaṉ irāmaṉ mutalil teṉṉāṭu pōnta poḻutu avaṉ akattiyaṉai riṣikaḷ (muṉikaḷ) kuḻuviṉariṭaiyē teṉṉāṭṭil kaṇṭatāka varukiṟatu; eṉavē kuṟainta aḷavu ki.mu. 1200 liruntē teṉṉintiya moḻiyākac tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyatu uṟutiyākiṟatu. antak kālak kaṭṭattilēyē tamiḻ uyariya ceyyuḷ naṭai vāynta centamiḻāka vaḻaṅkiyatākai yāl, ataṟku muṉṉarum mikap pala āṇṭukaḷ potu makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakku moḻiyākat tamiḻ iruntirukkavēṇṭum.

9. tamiḻ nāṭṭavarākiya perumpulavar, aṟiñaruḷ talai ciṟantavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ centamiḻ vaḻakkut tantaiyākiya akattiyar appoḻutu tamiḻē aṅkum nāṭṭu moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya vaṭa nāṭṭil iruntuvantavar eṉṟum, aṅkē camaskirutattaiyum avar kaṟṟuk koṇṭār eṉṟum karutu kiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa intiyāvil iṉṟum vāḻntu varum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum tamiḻukkum iṭaiyil uḷḷa nerukkampaṟṟi aṇmaiyil veḷivantuḷḷa uṇmaikaḷ avarkaḷ karuttai ātarikkiṉṟaṉa; eṉavē aṉaittintiyāvilum paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya ātimoḻi tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉpatu valuvāka niṟuvappaṭukiṟatu. (Tamil was the aboriginal language of all India). intukkaḷ [intō āriya moḻikku mutaṉmai tantavarkaḷ eṉṟa poruḷil] vaṭamēṟku intiyāviliruntu toṭaṅki innāṭṭu ātikkuṭikaḷai veṉṟu tam kīḻp paṭuttiyatu pōla, moḻittuṟaiyilum vaṭanāṭṭil paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ moḻiyaiyum tam kīḻppaṭuttiṉar; avar kaḷuṭaiya ittakaiya ātikkam vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum vaṭa intiyāvilum ēṟattāḻa muḻumaipeṟṟatu; āṉāl teṉṉintiyāvil appaṭiyillai.

10. mēṟcoṉṉataṟku viviliyam 1 irājākkaḷ X 22 -l varum

“22. rājāvukkuc camuttirattilē īrāmiṉ kappal kaḷōṭē kūṭat tar ṣīciṉ kappalkaḷum iruntatu; tarṣīciṉ kappalkaḷ mūṉṟu varuṣattukku oru taram poṉṉaiyum veḷḷiyaiyum yāṉait tantaṅkaḷaiyum (Shen habbim Habb iṉpaṟkaḷ”) kuraṅkukaḷaiyum, mayilkaḷaiyum koṇṭu eṉṉum vacaṉattil ātāram uḷḷatu. avvacaṉattil kuṟittuḷḷa aintu poruḷkaḷumē orē iṭattil oru cērak kiṭaikkum iṭam ilaṅkai - teṉṉintiyā maṭṭumē; vēṟu eṅkumalla. aṅku mayilaik kuṟikkum tuki (tōkai) tūyatamiḻccol, accol camaskirutattilum illai; tamiḻiya moḻiyallāta vēṟu intiya moḻi etilum illai. (camaskiruta Sikhi  tāṉ itu eṉkiṉṟaṉar cilamoḻiyiyalāḷar; āṉāl anca camaskirutac collai ciki eṉṟa vaṭivil piṟkālattil tamiḻum kaṭaṉ peṟṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. tōkaiyum cikiyum oru poruḷ paṟṟiya vevvēṟu coṟkaḷ eṉpatu tamiḻaṟiñar evarukkum teriyum) kapi eṉṉum epirēyac col (ape eṉṟu potuvāka viviliya āṅkilap peyarppukaḷ kuṟitta pōtilum) kuraṅkaik kuṟikkat tamiḻil appaṭiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟatu; atu camaskirutattilum uḷḷatu. āyiṉum cālamoṉ kālattiṟku muṉṉarē camaskirutam teṉṉintiyāviṟku vantuviṭṭatu ākaiyāl tamiḻ mūlaccollē epirēyattil ēṟiyuḷḷatu eṉalām. ‘tōkai’ tamiḻ ākaiyāl kapiyum tamiḻākavē irukkum. mēṟcoṉṉa vacaṉattil varum ‘kapi’ [ekiptiya moḻiyil Kaf - ape eṉpatāl] āṅkilattil ape eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭapōtilum ‘kuraṅku’ eṉpatē cari. tamiḻil kapi kuraṅkaiyē kuṟikkum. ‘kapi’ camaskirutattilum ēṟiyuḷḷatu. cālamaṉ kālattukku muṉṉālēyē camaskirutam teṉ intiyāviṟkuḷ - vantuviṭṭapaṭiyāl atu nikaḻn tirukkalām. tūyatamiḻc collāṉa ‘kapi’ yaiyē (tōkai pōṉṟu) epirēyam payaṉpaṭuttukiṟatu eṉak karutalām. yāṉait tantattaik kuṟippiṭa Shen habbim = habb ṉ paṟkaḷ (tooth of elephants) eṉṟa col varukiṟatu. yāṉaiyaik kuṟikka tamiḻilum camskirutattilum muṟaiyē ipam Ibham eṉṉum coṟkaḷum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ipporuḷkaḷai yellām ennāṭṭiliruntu tarṣīs kappalkaḷil cālamaṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉpataiyum, emmoḻiyēpacum makkaḷiṭam iruntu avaṟṟaik koḷmutal ceytu vantāṉ eṉpataiyum terivippaṉavāka ivvivaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

11. teṉṉintiyāviṉ tolkuṭikaḷiṉ moḻi tamiḻē eṉpatai nilaināṭṭa vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷum uḷa. teṉṉāṭṭu ūrppeyarkaḷ, nilattiṇaip peyarkaḷ ellām
(camaskirutattuṭaṉ toṭarpēyillāta) tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa. pirāmaṇiya camaya ātikkattāl appeyarkaḷuḷ pala camaskirutac coṟkaḷākap piṟkālattil māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; ālavāy - maturai; mukavai - irāmanātapuram; kallaṇai- rāmacētu; malaiyāḷam -tiruvāṅkūr. (paḻantamiḻt tol kuṭiyiṉariṉ oru pakutiyiṉarāṉa cāṇār perum eṇṇikkaiyil vāḻntu varum) Tinnevelly eṉpatum uṇmaiyil tirunelvēli eṉṉum tūya tamiḻppeyarē. Point Calimere eṉpatum tamiḻk kōṭikkaraiyē.

12. mēṟcoṉṉa ceytikaḷai “tamiḻ cemittiya moḻikkuṭumpattaic cārntatu” eṉṟa nilaimaiyai (mēlum āyvukaḷai nikaḻttiyapiṉ) nilai nāṭṭuvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷākak koḷḷalāmeṉakkarutukiṟēṉ.

13. tamiḻil aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ perumpālum viṉaikaḷē. avviṉaiccol vērkaḷ mūṉṟu eḻuttu, iraṇṭu acai Triliteral and disyllabic āka amaintuḷḷaṉa. cilavērkaḷil iraṇṭu eḻuttukaḷē uḷḷaṉa; iraṇṭu acaikaḷukku atikamākak koṇṭa vērccoṟkaḷ mikac cilavē

14. cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷai epirēyac coṟkaḷōṭu oppiṭumpoḻutu irumoḻic coṟkaḷum orē vēriliruntu tōṉṟiṉavō eṉak karutat takka aḷavukku ottuḷḷaṉa; irumoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum oppiṭṭapoḻutu kaṇṭa,piṉ varum iṇaikaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  (pattuccoṟkaḷai oppiṭṭāl avaṟṟil oṉṟu ittakaiya oppumai uḷḷatāka amaintatu). ittakaiya oppumaic coṟkaḷ mēlum pala irukka vēṇṭum.






tamiḻ 			epirēyam

pāri 			to produce, to form 	to create	
aṟa (aṟu) 		to reap,		to reap, to pluck
ēṟa 			to ascend /increase	to be high

māyvu 		death			death

[cemittiya moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷōṭu uṟavuṭaiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷāka 1856 l kālṭuvel tamoppilakkaṇattil kuṟittavai varumāṟu: appā, ammā, āṟu, al, avā, iru, iṟaṅku, eri, ūr, eṟi, erumai, kūr, cāy, ciṉam, ciṟu, cumai, cuvar, cevvai, nāṭṭu, nīṭṭu, nōkki, paḻu, pāl (pakuti) peṟu, vā (kaṉṉaṭattilum, pāpilōṉiyattilum ba epirēyattil bo) māy, māṟu, mettai.]

15. eṉṟu muḷa iṟaiyai eternal god kuṟikka tamiḻar atu eṉṉum collaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. pottām potuvāka iṟaiyai undeveloped or unorganized deity kuṟikkum tamiḻc coṟkaḷil ‘atu’ vum oṉṟu ākum. aṟiñar laut Lowth iṉ piṉvarum karuttu ittuṭaṉ oppu nōkkattakkatākum “epirēya moḻiyil avaṉ [tamiḻil ‘avar’ eṉalām] eṉpatu pala iṭaṅkaḷil meyyāṉa karttarai (tēvaṉai) kuṟikkiṟatu. upākamam Deuteronomy xxxii. 39; ēcāyā xiii. 10,13; XIviii.12; kuṟippāka caṅkītam Cii. 27”



tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ taṉmaikaḷ

16. tamiḻ viṉaiccoṟkaḷ kāṭṭum kālaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu: iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu. iṭam, eṇ, pāl ākiyavaṟṟai viṉaic col vikutikaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. (pālvēṟupāṭṭai (i)paṭarkkai uyartiṇai orumai viṉaiyum, (ii)paṭarkkai aḵṟiṇaippaṉmai viṉaiyum maṭṭumē kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ (eḻuvāyākiya patilippeyar coṟṟoṭarukku muṉṉar karantu niṟpatākak koḷka):-

naṭakkiṟēṉ		 I walk
naṭakkiṟāy/rīr 	Thou walkest
naṭakkiṟāṉ		 He walks
naṭakkiṟāḷ 		She walks
naṭakkiṉṟatu 		It walks
naṭakkiṟōm 		We walk
naṭakkiṟīr/ṟīrkaḷ 	Ye walk
naṭakkiṟārkaḷ 	They walk
naṭakkiṉṟaṉa		 Things walk

mukkāla viṉaiccoṟkaḷukkum viṉai vikutikaḷ ōrē mātirittāṉ uḷḷaṉa. aḵniṇai varuṅkālattil maṭṭum māṟṟam uṇṭu:-

naṭakkiṟēṉ	 I walk
naṭantēṉ 	I walked
naṭappēṉ	 I will Walk
naṭakkum	 It will walk

17. tamiḻ viḷaic colliṉ pāṅku mood aintu vakaiyil amaiyum; viṉai nilaiyuraittal, ēval, ceyal eñcuviṉai, viyaṅkōḷ, karuttuppuṉaiviyal pāṅku (ippaṭi naṭakkumāyiṉ/ naṭantuviṭṭāl eṉpatu pōl) indicative, imperative, infinitive, Optative, Subjunctive) kaṭaici mūṉṟu pāṅkukaḷum mutaṟpāṅku vaṭivattaip pala vaḻikaḷil caṟṟē māṟṟi uruvākkappaṭum. viyaṅkōḷ perumpālum varuṅkāla viṉaiyoṭṭōṭu āka ( = to be, to become iṉ ceyal eñcuviṉaivaṭivam) cērttu uruvākkappaṭum.

naṭappēṉāka		 May I walk or let me walk
naṭappīrāka		 Mayst thou walk etc
naṭappārkaḷāka 	May they walk etc

ēval viṉaikkup pala vaṭivaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. orumaiyil viṉaiyiṉ vērē ēval āka amaiyum. paṉmaiyil antanta iṭappaṉmaikkuriyap patilippeyar īṟai vēr uṭaṉ cērttu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.

naṭa		 Walk (thou)
naṭavuṅkaḷ 	Walk (Ye)

naṭavuṅkaḷ (naṭa + v + uṅkaḷ) eṉpatil varum v āṉatu ṭa (ṭ + a) viṉ iṟuti uyirukkum u eṉṉum uyirukkum iṭaiyil, viṭṭicai nīkkattiṟkāka maṭṭum (to prevent hiatus) varum meyyeḻuttākum. vēṟu cila iṭarkaḷil v kku patilāka y um varum.

18. tamiḻ moḻiyil iṇaippiṭap (patilip) peyarkaḷ Relative Pronouns illai. appatilip peyarkaḷ ceyyum vēlaiyai tamiḻil peyareccam ceykiṟatu. “vanta kutirai” the horse which came eṉṉum poḻutu “vanta” vil varum ‘a’ eṉpatē, aṭuttu varum “kutirai” tāṉ “vanta” eṉṉum ceyalaic ceytatueṉpatait terivittu viṭukiṟatu, peyareccattai āṅkilattil peyarkkum poḻutu oru viṉai muṟṟum, oru Relative Pronoun  um uruvākiviṭum.

naṭakkiṟa (‘naṭa’ viṉ nikaḻkālap peyareccam) maṉucaṉ = the man who walks / is walking

āṅkilac coṟṟoṭaraip pōlavē tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarum teḷivākavum tiṭṭavaṭṭamākavumamaintuḷḷatu.


19. viṉaic collukkup palavaḻikaḷil etirmaṟai vaṭivaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. viṉaiyiṉ vērōṭu nēraṭiyāka patilippeyar īṟukaḷai (ēṉ, āy, āṉ āṉ, āḷ
pōṉṟavaṟṟaic cērttuviṭutal :

naṭavēṉ = I will not walk
naṭavāy = Thou wilt not walk
naṭavāṉ = he will not walk etc

etirmaṟai viṉaiccollai “ceyal eñcuviṉai” vaṭivattiṉ iṟutiyil illai (no, not) eṉpataic cērttum uruvākkalām:-

naṭakka (=to walk) - naṭakkavillai = do, did not Walk

im muṟaiyai mūviṭaṅkaḷukkum orumai – paṉmai iraṇṭukkum appaṭiyē piṉpaṟṟalām. 

ivviraṇṭu etirmaṟai vaṭivaṅkaḷum kālam kāṭṭum vakai varumāṟu:

mutal vaṭivam (naṭavēṉ) - perum pālum varuṅkālam

iraṇṭāvatu vaṭivam (naṭakkavillai) – perum pālum iṟanta kālam

āyiṉum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷil etaṟkum payaṉpaṭuttalām.

20. etirmaṟaip peyareccattiṉ iṟutiyil “irukkiṟatu” (to be) pōṉṟa iṉṉoru viṉaic collaic cērttum etirmaṟai viṉaic collai uruvākkalām. appaṭi uruvākkum collil etirmaṟaippeyareccam māṟātu; aṭuttu varum viṉaic col maṭṭumē kālaṅkāṭṭum vakaiyil māṟṟam aṭaiyum:

naṭavātirukkiṟēṉ 		I do not walk
naṭavātiruntēṉ 		I did not walk
naṭavātiruppēṉ 		I will not walk
ivaiyellām cātāraṇat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. pulamaic centamiḻ naṭaiyil vēṟu muṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

21. aṉaittu viṉaikaḷiṉ “ceyal eñcuviṉai” varuṅkālam kāṭṭum vaṭiviliruntu ceyappāṭṭuviṉai Causative form uruvākkappaṭukiṟatu. ceyappāṭṭu viṉai ceyviṉai pōṉṟē muḻumai vāynta viṉaiyākaamaintu, atu pōlavē viṉai vikaṟpamum Conjugation  aṭaikiṟatu.

naṭappikkiṟēṉ 		I cause to walk
naṭappikkiṟāy 		Thou causest to walk
naṭakkiṟāṉ 			He causes to walk, etc, etc

cila viṉaic coṟkaḷ iṉṉoru muṟaiyil ceyappāṭṭuviṉai vaṭivam amaikkavum iṭantarukiṉṟaṉa;

naṭattukiṟēṉ 		I cause (any business - matter) to
walk - i.e. to advance
cumakkiṟēṉ 			I bear (a burden etc)
cumattukiṟēṉ		I cause (a burden) to be borne

iv vaṭivamum piṟaviṉaikaḷaip pōla aṉaittu iṭaṅkaḷ, kālaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟukkāṉa viṉai vikaṟpaṅkaḷaiyum aṭaikiṟatu. iṟutiyākac coṉṉa ceyappāṭṭu viṉai vaṭivattiṉ mūlam ‘ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟum viṉaiyai’ ce.po. kuṉṟāviṉaiyākkalām.

varukiṟēṉ	 	I come
varuttukiṟēṉ	 I cause to come

22. kuṟippu viṉai eṉṉum (Appellative verb)kaḷ ērāḷamāṉavai tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. avai potuvāṉa peyarc coṟkaḷaip pōla vēṟṟumai urupēṟṟal mutaliya peyart tiripukaḷai aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippu viṉaiyiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa Symbolic verbs tamiḻil kāṇappaṭum taṉi vakai viṉaic coṟkaḷ ākum. avai perum pālum pulamai naṭait tamiḻilēyē varukiṉṟaṉa. cilavērc coṟkaḷ, ippoḻutu aṭaikaḷāka adjectives maṭṭum payaṉpaṭum paḻaiya peyarc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu avviṉaic coṟkaḷ amaikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum entap peyarc collil iruntum avaṟṟai uruvākkalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka.

aṭi (step, foot, root, servitude - aṭiyēṉ I you
Servant uṭai (possession) - uṭaiyēṉ I the
Possessor 

ivaṟṟai aṉaittu iṭam, eṇ, pālkaḷilum payaṉ paṭuttalām.

23. eḻuvāyaiyum ceyappaṭuporuḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya orē collai potuvāṉa ēṉaip peyarc coṟkaḷaip pōla kaiyāḷalām.

koṭiyai ciṟiyēṉai aṭittāy thou who art a cruel
manhast beaten me who am a small man 

“koṭiyai”eṉpatu kūṭṭuccol eḻuvāy; ‘ciṟiyēṉai’
eṉpatu kūṭṭuc col ceyappaṭuporuḷ - ai eṉṉum
vēṟṟumai urupai ēṟṟuḷḷatu. ‘aṭittāy’
potuvāṉa iṟantakāla viṉaimuṟṟu.

24. tamiḻ viṉaiccoṟkaḷil uḷḷa iṉṉoru ciṟappuk kūṟaiyum kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum. atu tāṉviṉaiyeccam (gerund, verbal participle, first indefinite mood) pala ceyalkaḷaip paṟṟiya oruvākkiyattil iṟuti viṉaiyaik kuṟikkum collait tavira ēṉaiya viṉaikaḷ viṉaiyeccamākavēvarum: 

nāḷaikku nām ceṉṟu, neṟpayirai aṟuttuk katirk
kaṭṭukaḷaik kaḷattukku uyttu, avaṟṟai aṭittu
nellākki, viṟṟuṇa, varikaḷaik kaṭṭuvōmāka

eṉṉum poḻutu muntaiya viṉaikaḷ ellām viṉaiyeccamāka irukka kaṭaici viṉai (kaṭṭuvōmāka) maṭṭumē viṉaimuṟṟāka iruppataik kāṇalām. viṉaimuṟṟai yotta atēkālam  , eṇ, iṭam mutaliyavai viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷukkum amaikiṉṟaṉa.

cuṭṭu (ciṟappu/ potumai) coṟkaḷ (Articles)

25. tamiḻil ivai illai. (The pōṉṟa ciṟappuc cuṭṭuc collukkup patilāka cuṭṭup patilip peyarkaḷ (ivaṉ, avaṉ, itu, atu) allatu vēṟu vakai vaḻakkukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (a, an pōṉṟa ) potumaic cuṭṭuc collukkup pakaramāka oru/ōr payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. 

vēṟṟumai urupēṟkum poḻutu peyarc coṟkaḷ aṭaiyum tiripukaḷ Declensions

26. tamiḻil peyarc coṟkaḷukku eṭṭu vēṟṟumaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. (oru cilavaṟṟait tavira) aṉaittup peyarc coṟkaḷukkum vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ potuvāṉavaiyē. avvēṟṟumaikaḷāl peyarccoṟkaḷ aṭaiyum poruḷ māṟṟattai muṟaiyē piṉvarumāṟu taralām: avaṉ He; avaṉai him; avaṉai / avaṉōṭu by or with him;avaṉukku To him; avaṉiliruntu from him, avaṉatu of him or his; avaṉil/ avaṉiṭattil at or in him; viḷivēṟṟumai Vocative

patilippeyarkaḷ Pronouns

27. mūviṭa naparkaḷaic cuṭṭum patilippeyarkaḷum, cila peyarccoṟkaḷum iraṇṭu vakaip paṉmai vaṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; avviru vakaikaḷumē cuṭṭappaṭuvāṉ uyarvaik kāṭṭap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. appaṉmaippeyarkaḷukkuriya viṉaic coṟkaḷum avaṟṟukku iyaipākavē amaikiṉṟaṉa:-

nāṉ I 	nām, nāṅkaḷ = We, allatu (mariyātai koṭuttuc
cuṭṭum) nāṉ

nī thou 	nīr, nīṅkaḷ = ye  allatu (mariyātai koṭuttuc
cuṭṭum) nī

avaṉ he 	avar, avarkaḷ = they allatu mariyātai koṭuttuc

avaḷ she	 cuṭṭum avaṉ / avaḷ

28. taṉmaippaṉmaiyil nām/ nāṅkaḷ ākiya coṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum poḻutu avaṟṟil aṭaṅku pavarkaḷ vēṟupaṭuvar 

nām 		= 	pēcupavaṉ, kēṭpavarkaḷ (nām ellām 
pāvikaḷ) We are all sinners
nāṅkaḷ 	= 	pēcupavaṉ maṭṭum (“nīṅkaḷ” mātiri)

29. kaṭavuḷiṭam ‘nī’ eṉṟu kūṟāmal ‘tēvarīr’ (= kaṭavuḷarkaḷākiya nīṅkaḷ) eṉṟu kūṟuvatum peruvaḻakkil uḷḷatu. (tēvar = kaṭavuḷ + īr (muṉṉilai patilippeyar paṉmai mariyātai vaṭivu) = tēvarīr. muṉṉilaip patilip peyarp paṉmaiyil ‘īr’ varuvatu pōla viṉaiyilum īrvarum (nīr vantīr)

kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikkum “cuvāmi” paṉmaip poruḷil (Pluralis excellentias) cuvāmiyār eṉṟu varum. paṭarkkaip paṉmaiyil viṉaiccol īṟākavarum ār, ārkaḷ (vantār, vantārkaḷ) eṉpavaṟṟil mutalvaṭivamākiya ār ippaṭi peyarccol īṟākaccērkkappaṭukiṟatu – mariyātaikkāka 

vākkiya amaippil coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭar kaḷum varum varicai

30. colloṭṭukaḷ yāvumē peyarc collukkup piṉṉar maṭṭumē varuvatāl tamiḻil avai piṉṉoṭṭukaḷē (Postpositions) ākum.

31. āṟām vēṟṟumaiyākiya uṭaimaip poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyil ‘avaṉatu māṭu’ eṉpatu pōl ‘atu’ eṉṉum vēṟṟumai urupu etu uṭaimai eṉac collappaṭukiṟatō (māṭu) ataṟku muṉṉar varum.

32. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉaiyil eḻuvāy, ceyappaṭuporuḷ ivaṟṟukkup piṉṉarē viṉaic col varum: nāṉ avaṉai aṭittēṉ (I him beat)
33. palavakaic coṟkaḷiṉ (Parts of speech) varicai muṟai āṅkilattil uḷḷataṟku ēṟattāḻat talaikīḻākavē irukkum.

“The man who came here yesterday”
= nēṟṟu iṅkē vantavaṉ
(yesterday here who came the man)

peyaraṭaikaḷ (Adjectives)

34. peyaraṭaikaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷukku muṉṉar varukiṉṟaṉa. pāl, eṇ, vēṟṟumai ivaṟṟukkēṟpa peyaraṭaikaḷ enta māṟṟamum peṟuvatillai.

35. oppumaiyaik kāṭṭavō, taṉicciṟappaik kāṭṭavō (Comparative, Superlative degree kaḷukku āṅkilattil uḷḷatu pōl peyaraṭaikaḷ tamiḻilil urumāṟṟam peṟuvatillai. oppumaiyaik kāṭṭum poḻutu nāṉkām/ aintām vēṟṟumai Dative / Ablative urupukaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti tamiḻil “ataṟku itu nallatu” eṉpatu pōl varum. (āṅkilattil ammoḻiyiṉ amaippukkēṟka) this is better than that eṉṟu varum poḻutu- “than that” il oppumaic col mutalil varum; āṉāl tamiḻil ataṟku eṉṟu oppumaic col īṟṟil varum)

36. “pārkka” eṉṉum viṉaic collaip payaṉ paṭuttiyum tamiḻil oppumai viḷakkappaṭum “ataippārkka itu nallatu” (to look at that/while we look at that, this is good)

37. taṉicciṟappāṉatu “ellāvaṟṟiṉum/ ellōriṉum” eṉṉum collaip payaṉpaṭutti aintām Ablative) vēṟṟumai urupuṭaṉ viḷakkappaṭum. “ellōriṉum kaṭavuḷ uyarntavar”; “aṉaittilum itu ciṟantatu.”

38. tamiḻiṉ piṟa taṉmaikaḷaiyum virivāka viḷakkalām. āyiṉum inta āyviṉ nōkkattiṟku itu pōkum. annōkkamāṉatu tamiḻ ciṟanta, mukkiyamāṉa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpatai aṟiñarkaḷukkut terivippatum, ammoḻiyai ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ vakaippāṭṭil evviṭattil varum eṉpatu kuṟittu avarkaḷ āyvu ceyya utavuvatumē ākum.

[aṭuttu āṅkila mūlamum tarappaṭukiṟatu ]
